# Editor requirements

This is the one source of what ThoughtKnot's editor must do. It is a graph note: the requirements graph is the one
`gql` block below, and the words around it are for people.

- **Change it here**, in a pull request. Add a requirement as soon as it is agreed.
- **A test only moves forward.** It goes from planned to partial to written, never back.
- **Every tool reads this note.** The checks, the slice board, the wiki and the GitHub mirror all read the block below.
  Run `node scripts/check-requirements.mjs` to check it.

```gql
// graph: editor_requirements · SOURCE
// The editor's requirements graph, the one source: edit it here, in a pull request. A test only moves forward.
// ── Sections ──
(section_1:Section {num: '1', title: 'Purpose and scope'}),
(section_2:Section {num: '2', title: 'One store, three layers'}),
(section_3:Section {num: '3', title: 'Viewing — the default'}),
(section_4:Section {num: '4', title: 'The edit pane — text'}),
(section_5:Section {num: '5', title: 'The edit pane — drawing'}),
(section_6:Section {num: '6', title: 'Nudges'}),
(section_7:Section {num: '7', title: 'Views and paths'}),
(section_8:Section {num: '8', title: 'Help that finds and fixes problems'}),
(section_9:Section {num: '9', title: 'Resilience'}),
(section_10:Section {num: '10', title: 'Hosts — one core, Obsidian first, VS Code later'}),
(section_11:Section {num: '11', title: 'Speed and accessibility'}),
(section_12:Section {num: '12', title: 'Testing'}),
(section_13:Section {num: '13', title: 'Delivery'}),
(section_14:Section {num: '14', title: 'Properties — shown inline and inspected'}),
(section_15:Section {num: '15', title: 'Diagram types'}),
(section_16:Section {num: '16', title: 'Export'}),
(section_17:Section {num: '17', title: 'AI proposals — accepted or rejected in the editor'}),
(section_18:Section {num: '18', title: 'Links and attachments'}),
(section_19:Section {num: '19', title: 'Working with an AI — the user in charge'}),
(section_20:Section {num: '20', title: 'Graph sources and graph steps'}),
(section_21:Section {num: '21', title: 'Settings — strictness and the other options'}),
(section_24:Section {num: '24', title: 'Viewer aids and notes on things'}),
(section_25:Section {num: '25', title: 'Finding your way round a large graph'}),
(section_26:Section {num: '26', title: 'The full editor in Claude Code\'s browser pane'}),
(section_27:Section {num: '27', title: 'Type, instance and the store underneath'}),
(section_28:Section {num: '28', title: 'Certainty and contested links'}),
(section_29:Section {num: '29', title: 'Time and provenance'}),
(section_30:Section {num: '30', title: 'Joining the mesh'}),
(section_31:Section {num: '31', title: 'Clusters and centroids'}),
(section_32:Section {num: '32', title: 'Altitude and audience profiles'}),
(section_33:Section {num: '33', title: 'Lenses as graph data'}),
(section_34:Section {num: '34', title: 'The process core and its views'}),
(section_35:Section {num: '35', title: 'Runs, tests and the process debugger'}),
(section_36:Section {num: '36', title: 'Certainty in the drawing, and the theme graph'}),
(section_37:Section {num: '37', title: 'The AI query contract and write scoring'}),
(section_38:Section {num: '38', title: 'Export, gaps and secrets'}),
(section_39:Section {num: '39', title: 'Permissions and personal profiles'}),
(section_40:Section {num: '40', title: 'Sync, devices and the corporate boundary'}),
(section_41:Section {num: '41', title: 'Every surface, released and checked automatically'}),
(section_42:Section {num: '42', title: 'Language, culture and direction'}),
(section_43:Section {num: '43', title: 'Plug-ins, layers and their contracts'}),
(section_44:Section {num: '44', title: 'Theming and the standard interface needs'}),
(section_45:Section {num: '45', title: 'Whiteboard: brainstorm on a board that knows the graph'}),
(section_46:Section {num: '46', title: 'A Copilot Cowork skill: graph notes in Microsoft 365'}),
(section_47:Section {num: '47', title: 'Text to graph: an AI breaks prose into a graph'}),
(section_48:Section {num: '48', title: 'Graphs kept in a GitHub repository'}),
(section_49:Section {num: '49', title: 'Claude presents a graph and takes decisions'}),
(section_50:Section {num: '50', title: 'Known risks: the register the release waits on'}),
(section_51:Section {num: '51', title: 'The graph spec: checked, versioned and complete'}),
(section_52:Section {num: '52', title: 'Data tables and cubes, from outside sources too'}),
(section_53:Section {num: '53', title: 'Massive graphs on your own machine'}),
(section_54:Section {num: '54', title: 'The trust model: who and what each part trusts'}),
(section_55:Section {num: '55', title: 'Storage of your choice: where your graphs are kept'}),
(section_56:Section {num: '56', title: 'Rules and looks: formatting by rule'}),
(section_57:Section {num: '57', title: 'Calculated values and the table view'}),
(section_58:Section {num: '58', title: 'Intents: what supports or contradicts a goal'}),
(section_59:Section {num: '59', title: 'AI suggestions for categories and intents'}),
(section_60:Section {num: '60', title: 'Discovery in large graphs'}),
(section_61:Section {num: '61', title: 'Contexts: where links may join'}),
// ── Requirements ──
(works_offline_everywhere:Requirement {section: '1', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must work in Obsidian on desktop and on mobile, offline.'}),
(text_is_the_store:Requirement {section: '2', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The text of the graph block is the only store of the graph.'}),
(edits_are_text:Requirement {section: '2', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Every graphic edit must be written as a change to the text of the graph block.'}),
(views_in_the_note:Requirement {section: '2', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Views must be stored as named query blocks in the note.'}),
(style_is_a_graph:Requirement {section: '2', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Nudges, the properties shown, and the styling of views and paths must be written as a style graph in the same notation, never in the data graph.'}),
(style_graph_checked:Requirement {section: '2', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'The style graph must be checked like any graph, and every thing it names must exist in the graph it styles.'}),
(style_words_checked:Requirement {section: '2', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A key the style graph does not know, or a frame that is not a well-formed list of numbers, must be reported as a problem with its fix; a view\'s name is the user\'s own and must never be checked against anything.'}),
(style_block_in_note:Requirement {section: '2', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'The style graph must be written as its own block in the same note as the graph it styles, and shown folded away until opened.'}),
(broken_style_block:Requirement {section: '2', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A style block that cannot be read must not stop the drawing: the graph is drawn in the locked style, and the problem listed.'}),
(drawn_where_written:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every graph block must be drawn in reading view and in Live Preview.'}),
(drawing_bar:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The drawing must carry a problems badge, Fit, Full-screen and Edit.'}),
(bar_and_details_belong:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The bar and the details shown on a tap must each be seen at a glance to belong to their own drawing, in light and dark themes and with several drawings in one note.'}),
(tap_link_for_details:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Tapping a link must show its reading, the things at either end, its properties, and any problem on it.'}),
(tap_for_details:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Tapping a box must show its text, its kind, its links in and out, and any problem on it.'}),
(draws_despite_problems:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A graph with problems must still be drawn: what can be read is drawn, and the rest is listed behind the badge.'}),
(drawn_in_part:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A block that cannot be read must be drawn in part, what of it can be read, by the plugin and the vault Python alike, byte for byte; checking and loading stay all or nothing.'}),
(one_whitespace_set:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Every reader must treat as whitespace exactly the set that GQL\'s grammar names, and end a line at LF only, so every reader reads and draws the same note the same way.'}),
(mini_map:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'When a drawing is bigger than its view, the viewer must offer a mini-map: a small copy of the whole drawing with the visible part outlined, which moves the view when tapped or dragged, in a card and in the pane alike.'}),
(mini_map_setting:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Whether the mini-map shows must be a setting: Off, When the drawing does not fit (the default), or Always.'}),
(mini_map_shape:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A tall or wide drawing must keep a mini-map of the whole drawing, never only the part on screen: its long side capped, and its short side kept to a set size while the whole drawing still fits (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(details_always_visible:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A setting, off by default, must let the details panel stay open beside or below the drawing, in a card and in the pane, showing the graph\'s summary while nothing is chosen.'}),
(drawing_height_setting:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'How tall a drawing in a note may be must be a setting: half the window, three quarters of it (the default), or no limit.'}),
(details_narrow:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'should', rule: 'Where a card is too narrow for the panel and the drawing side by side, the panel should go below the drawing.'}),
(quick_info:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Pointing at a thing or a link for a moment must show a tooltip in the theme\'s style, giving its name, its kind, its first few properties, how many problems it has and the first paragraph of its note.'}),
(quick_info_links:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A thing\'s tooltip must say how it is linked, under its name, in the words its details open with, so the two never disagree.'}),
(quick_info_read_aloud:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A thing or link that has the keyboard focus must be described by its open tooltip, so a screen reader reads it, and no longer once the tooltip closes.'}),
(quick_info_no_hover_only:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The tooltip must also open by long-press on touch and when the thing has keyboard focus, and must never cover the thing itself.'}),
(note_comment:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A note on a thing or a link must be one or more lines of their own that start with exactly three slashes followed by a space or the end of the line, directly above the line that holds it; a blank line or an ordinary comment in between ends the note, but a tagged comment line that carries a key (§27 of the graph spec) does not: it sits inside the note\'s run.'}),
(note_text:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A note\'s text must be what follows the three slashes and a space on each line, joined in order, with a bare /// line making a paragraph break, and it must be read as CommonMark Markdown that the host renders.'}),
(note_never_other_comments:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Lines of two slashes or of four or more, -- and /* */ comments, and the // graph: and // excerpt lines must never be notes.'}),
(note_one_owner:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A note above a line holding more than one thing must belong to none of them, and the editor must say so, with the fix of putting the thing on its own line.'}),
(note_both_readers:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The JavaScript and Python readers must read notes alike, proved by shared parity cases.'}),
(note_shown:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A thing\'s note must show in its details and its tooltip, rendered as Markdown.'}),
(note_callouts:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A setting must draw notes on the drawing as note-shaped callouts joined to their thing by a dashed line, off by default.'}),
(note_kept_by_edits:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Every edit the editor makes, including moving, reformatting, renaming and deleting, must keep a thing\'s note with it and delete the note with the thing, and undo must restore both.'}),
(note_add:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The details panel must offer to add, change and remove a thing\'s note, writing the comment lines above it.'}),
(note_standard:Requirement {section: '24', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Notes must stay plain GQL comments, so engines and the standard checker ignore them, and exports must keep them where the target format has comments and leave them out where it has none.'}),
(one_locked_style:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every drawing must use the locked style sidecar, graph_svg.style.json'}),
(zoom_buttons_and_gestures:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every drawing must offer Fit, zoom in, zoom out and Full-screen, as buttons and as gestures.'}),
(pinch_to_zoom:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'On touch, pinching must zoom about the point between the fingers, and dragging must pan once zoomed in.'}),
(wheel_and_trackpad:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'On desktop, a trackpad pinch, or the mouse wheel with Ctrl or Command held, must zoom about the pointer, and dragging empty space must pan.'}),
(pan_freely:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Dragging must pan the drawing freely, past its edges, and never stop because an edge has come into view; only a strip of it stays in sight, so it is never lost, and Fit must bring the whole of it back.'}),
(note_still_scrolls:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'In a note, the mouse wheel alone must scroll the note, never zoom the drawing; in full-screen and in the edit pane it must zoom.'}),
(double_tap_zoom:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Double-clicking or double-tapping empty space must zoom in there, and Fit must return to the whole drawing.'}),
(zoom_keys:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The keys plus, minus and zero must zoom in, zoom out and fit when the drawing has focus, and Escape must leave full-screen.'}),
(zoom_limits:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Zoom must never go beyond four times natural size, nor further out than a quarter of it (from slice 3, no further out than the fit of §25), and text must stay sharp at every level.'}),
(one_zoom:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must replace the Mermaid Zoom plugin for graph drawings, so a drawing never has two sets of zoom controls.'}),
(note_height_capped:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'In a note, a drawing must never be taller than the share of the window its setting allows: a taller one is fitted inside it, so its details and the rest of the note stay in reach; full screen and the pane keep their own size.'}),
(estimate_near_name:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A link that names a thing not in the graph, where exactly one thing in the graph is near that name, must be drawn to that thing as an estimate, dashed and marked with a warning.'}),
(estimate_fix_on_tap:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 2, level: 'should', rule: 'Tapping an estimated link should select it as any link is selected, and its details should offer its fix in one press, writing what it was taken to mean exactly as the problem’s own fix does and only into the text as drawn, and Edit the link, which opens the editor with the misspelt name selected; neither is offered where the note is only read.'}),
(estimate_how_near:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Near means a difference of one letter for a name under eight characters and two for a longer one, ignoring case and underscores, as the graph library counts near duplicates; a name shorter than three characters must never be estimated.'}),
(estimate_near_type:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The same holds for a relationship type near exactly one type in the graph.'}),
(near_misses_only:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A link must never be estimated from the pattern of the graph, only from a near miss of a name or type.'}),
(estimate_never_written:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'An estimate must never be written to the data until it is accepted.'}),
(no_guess_between_equals:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Where two or more things are equally close, the link must not be drawn, and the problem must list them.'}),
(estimate_explains:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Tapping an estimated link must explain it in a sentence: what was written, what it was taken to mean, and the fix.'}),
(own_pane:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Edit must open a pane beside the note, on the block that was tapped.'}),
(block_picker:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A note with several graph blocks must offer a picker in the pane.'}),
(same_colours:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The text must be coloured with the same tokenizer as the gql-highlight plugin.'}),
(problems_in_place:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every problem must be marked where it is in the text, with its fix offered in place.'}),
(suggest_while_typing:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Names, kinds and relationships already in the graph must be suggested while typing.'}),
(comma_added_for_you:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'the missing comma must be added automatically while typing, in the pane and in the note, and one undo must take it back.'}),
(comma_never_rewritten_unasked:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A note that nobody is editing must never be rewritten in the background: its drawing must read the line break as the comma, and the fix must be offered in place.'}),
(suggest_brings_colon:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A suggested relationship or kind must bring its colon, so it never has to be typed.'}),
(edit_in_place:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The text must also be editable inside the drawing’s own card in the note, with the same help as the pane.'}),
(edit_button_toggles:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'While the card is being edited, its edit button must show a view icon, and pressing it must close the edit box exactly as Done does.'}),
(long_lines_wrap:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A line of text longer than the box must wrap onto the next row, in the pane and in place, its colours and problem marks wrapping with it; the plain-language note at the end of a line must never push the text out of step with the field, and the suggestions must drop down under the caret on the row it is on.'}),
(inline_untouched_by_theme:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The in-place box must look the same whether hovered, focused or neither, and its accessible name must not raise a tooltip.'}),
(reading_view_read_only:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'In Reading view, the bar must carry a Source switch in place of Edit, showing the block text read-only and coloured instead of the drawing, and back; nothing in Reading view may edit the block.'}),
(reading_view_whole_note:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Nothing the plugin offers in Reading view may change the note: a command must act only on the note in the pane in front, and must refuse, saying why, while that pane is in Reading view or holds no note; a graph embedded in another note must be read-only wherever it is shown; and an edit pane keeps saving even while another pane shows the same note in Reading view.'}),
(inline_editing_view_only:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Editing in place must be offered in Live Preview and Source mode, and never in Reading view.'}),
(page_stays_still:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'While the card is edited, the note must not scroll by itself: every write, redraw and rebuild of the card must leave the box being typed in at the same place on screen.'}),
(typing_keeps_focus:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every edit must redraw the drawing, and the box being typed in must keep focus, caret, selection, scroll and undo history through every redraw.'}),
(colon_added_for_you:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A link type typed without its colon, matching exactly one type of the graph, must have the colon added while typing, in the pane and in the note, as missing commas are.'}),
(many_views_one_data:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Several views of the same graph must be able to be shown, in one note or in several, each drawn from the one data set and none copying it.'}),
(view_of_another_note:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'A view in one note should be able to draw a graph whose data is written in another note, read where it is written and never copied; the drawing must say which note the data comes from, and open it on tap.'}),
(tap_cycles_near:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 2, level: 'should', rule: 'A tap on or near one or more boxes or links should select the nearest, and tapping again in the same place should select the next, round to the first; a tap near things is never half of a double-tap zoom.'}),
(schema_drawn:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A block that holds only a graph\'s schema, its own or a shared one, must be drawn by default as the schema itself: each kind of thing a box, each relationship type an arrow from the kinds it joins in the data to the kinds it reaches, labelled with its reading, and a relationship type the data does not use yet a box of its own, said so. The card must be read-only, with Source showing the text, and drawing it must write nothing to the note.'}),
(shown_text_ranked:Requirement {section: '3', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A box must show the first of these that gives text: the property its kind\'s schema names with shown_by; name, title, text or label; statement, summary, heading, question or description; the first readable text written, with words and a space and not a link; then its id. Text over 80 characters is cut at a word and ends in …. Both libraries choose alike (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(select_moves_text:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 2, level: 'should', rule: 'When editing, selecting a box or a link in the drawing should move the text to where it is written: the pattern that writes a thing, or else its first mention, and a link by its brackets and arrow.'}),
(gql_file_source:Requirement {section: '20', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A graph may be written in a .gql file, and a viewer block in a note must be able to draw it from that file, read where it is written and never copied.'}),
(plugin_step_named:Requirement {section: '20', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A step block must be able to name a step a plug-in adds by its id, such as example.sample.chain, with what it takes and no rule. Its inputs must be checked first, and what the plug-in gives back offered as a proposal; with no plug-in turned on that adds it, Run must say so.'}),
(plugin_source_from:Requirement {section: '20', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A viewer block must be able to draw its graph from a plug-in’s source, written as the source’s word and its place after FROM. With no plug-in turned on that adds the source, the block must say so and draw nothing.'}),
(gql_file_missing:Requirement {section: '20', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'If the file is missing, moved or unreadable, the viewer must say which file and why, and never show a blank drawing.'}),
(db_import_on_request:Requirement {section: '20', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must import from a SQLite database file in the vault only when the user presses Import, on desktop and mobile alike, reading it on the device, writing the result into a note as text and keeping no live link (Roger, 20 Oct 2026).'}),
(graph_step_io:Requirement {section: '20', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A step in a graph must be able to take a graph, or part of one such as a queried path, as an input at a named point, and give a graph as its output.'}),
(step_inputs_checked:Requirement {section: '20', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Each input must be checked against what the step declares it takes, before the step runs.'}),
(run_declared_only:Requirement {section: '20', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A step must be written as a query or a declared rule over graph data, never as code or a named action, so the editor only ever works out information.'}),
(step_output_proposal:Requirement {section: '20', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'An output graph must be shown as a proposal and written only when a person accepts it (§17).'}),
(settings_page:Requirement {section: '21', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'The plugin must have one settings page, in the plugin settings of Obsidian, holding every option the editor offers, each with a sentence saying what it does.'}),
(strictness_levels:Requirement {section: '21', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Strictness must be a setting with three levels: Standard, where every departure from standard GQL is an error; Forgiving, the default, where a slip is read, reported and fixed; and Simplified, where the departures chosen on the page are allowed as written.'}),
(simplified_back_to_standard:Requirement {section: '21', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'In Simplified, one action must convert a note back to standard GQL, checked by the standard checker before it is written.'}),
(engines_get_standard:Requirement {section: '21', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Whatever the setting, an engine or an export must receive standard GQL.'}),
(settings_ai_models:Requirement {section: '21', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'The AI models of §19, each with its purpose and its ceiling, must be set up on this page.'}),
(settings_formatting:Requirement {section: '21', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'How the editor formats the text it writes, and its drawing defaults, must be set on this page, choosing only among what the locked style allows.'}),
(settings_db_connections:Requirement {section: '21', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Import must need no setup and keep no secret: it offers the SQLite files in the vault, and no connection, password or key is kept anywhere (Roger, 20 Oct 2026).'}),
(rich_details:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The details of a thing must show its text and properties as Markdown rendered by the host, so images, maths, links and embeds appear as they would in a note.'}),
(node_is_a_note:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'A thing should be able to stand for a note: its id names the note, as a global note id does (§10 of the graph spec), its details show that note rendered, and opening the thing opens the note.'}),
(remote_images_asked:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'An image from outside the vault must not be loaded into the details until the user asks for it, so that showing a graph never sends a request on its own (§13).'}),
(animated_image_shown:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A thing must be able to show an animated image, whether a GIF, an APNG, an animated WebP or an animated SVG, in its details and, where its style asks, as a picture on its box.'}),
(animation_pause_play:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Every animated image must have a pause and play control, reachable from the keyboard.'}),
(animated_image_limited:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'An animated image larger than the size limit must be shown as its first frame, saying why, so that a large image never slows the drawing.'}),
(export_animation_stated:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'An export must say what it did with each animated image: kept it moving where the format allows, reduced it to its first frame, or left it out when no first frame can be made.'}),
(motion_reduced_respected:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'When the system asks for reduced motion, every animated image and every moving link must start still, and move only when the user plays it.'}),
(links_show_flow:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'A link should be able to show flow by moving: dashes running along its arrow, or a dot travelling a walked path.'}),
(flow_in_style_graph:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Whether and how links move must be set in the style graph, never in the data, and must be off by default.'}),
(motion_not_alone:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Movement must never be the only signal: a moving link also carries words or an icon saying what the movement means.'}),
(path_autoplay:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Play must be able to step through a walked path on its own, at a pace the user sets, with a moving highlight on the current step, and must pause or stop at any step.'}),
(path_animation_export:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'A played path should be able to be exported as an animated SVG, so that no encoder library is needed.'}),
(animation_export_fits:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'An animated SVG export too large for the device must be refused before it starts, saying why and what would make it fit.'}),
(view_saved_as_shown:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'should', rule: 'A view should be able to save how it is shown, when the user asks: its zoom, its position, the area framed and what is selected, written in the style graph and never in the data.'}),
(read_back:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'should', rule: 'Each line should show a plain-language reading beside it.'}),
(phone_tabs:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'On a phone the pane must offer Drawing and Text as two tabs, with forms and problems in a bottom sheet.'}),
(name_from_text:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'When a thing is given text but no name, a name must be suggested from the text, following the pattern of the names already in the graph.'}),
(text_from_name:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'When a thing is given a name but no text, text must be suggested from the name'}),
(suggested_name_unique:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A suggested name must be unique in the graph, and must never be used without being shown first.'}),
(autocomplete:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 2, level: 'should', rule: 'The editor should complete as you type, in the pane and in the card: brackets, braces and quotes; keywords; declared kinds and types; property names and values already used. The best match is offered inline, accepted with Tab or Enter, dismissed with Escape, never inserted unasked, and one undo takes it back.'}),
(add_thing:Requirement {section: '5', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Adding a thing must ask for its text first and suggest its name from it'}),
(drag_to_link:Requirement {section: '5', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Dragging from the handle of a box to another box must create a link, chosen by its reading.'}),
(remove_lists_links:Requirement {section: '5', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Removing a thing must first list the links that go with it, and undo must restore all of them.'}),
(highlight_written_line:Requirement {section: '5', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'After a graphic edit, the line written must be highlighted in the text.'}),
(park_for_later:Requirement {section: '5', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Marking a thing for later must write a Deferred saying what is needed and why it waits.'}),
(deferred_drawn:Requirement {section: '5', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Deferred things must be drawn dashed with a later mark.'}),
(quick_to_note:Requirement {section: '5', slice: 1, level: 'should', rule: 'A change should reach the note within about a third of a second.'}),
(touch_targets:Requirement {section: '5', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Touch targets must be at least 44 points, and no action may depend on hover.'}),
(not_dragging_alone:Requirement {section: '5', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Every action done by dragging must also be possible without dragging, from a menu or a form.'}),
(long_press_drag:Requirement {section: '5', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'On a phone, moving a box must start with a long press, so scrolling the drawing and moving a box never clash.'}),
(no_nudges_layout_places:Requirement {section: '6', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'With no nudges, the layout must place every box.'}),
(four_nudge_words:Requirement {section: '6', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A nudge must be one of four words: beside, before, group or direction.'}),
(nudge_preview:Requirement {section: '6', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Dragging a box must show where it would land and the nudge it would write before it is let go.'}),
(rename_carries_nudges:Requirement {section: '6', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A rename made in the editor must rewrite every reference to the thing, in the style graph and in view queries, in the same step.'}),
(nudge_problems:Requirement {section: '6', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A nudge that names nothing, or that conflicts with another, must be reported as a problem with a fix.'}),
(python_honours_nudges:Requirement {section: '6', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'The Python drawing script must honour nudges the same way.'}),
(drag_moves_box:Requirement {section: '6', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Dragging a box with a mouse or pen must move it: let go where its preview shows a place, it must write that nudge to the style graph and say so; Escape, or letting go where no place is shown, must write nothing; and dragging empty space must still pan.'}),
(hold_to_drag_touch:Requirement {section: '6', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'On touch, a box must be picked up by pressing and holding it before dragging, so that a plain swipe still scrolls the note or pans the drawing.'}),
(move_box_keyboard:Requirement {section: '6', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A box must be movable without dragging: from the keyboard (M picks the selected box up, the arrow keys step through the places it can go, Enter writes the nudge and Escape cancels) and from a Move choice in its details, each saying the nudge it would write before it is written.'}),
(radial_spoke_order:Requirement {section: '6', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'In a radial drawing, moving a box must change its order around the centre, written as a before nudge, and the radial layout must follow the before nudges for that order, the same in the core and in graph_svg.py.'}),
(view_is_query:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A view must be a query block whose header names it as a view'}),
(five_view_kinds:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Five kinds of view must be offered: a hand-picked set, everything near a thing, how one thing reaches another, only some kinds, and a walked path.'}),
(views_by_tapping:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A view must be built by tapping, and the editor must write its query and show its reading in plain language.'}),
(view_profile:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A view query outside the view profile must be refused with a message saying what is allowed.'}),
(views_agree_with_grafeo:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Every view must return the same things in the editor as in Grafeo.'}),
(fade_or_hide_in_style:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Whether things outside a view are faded or hidden must be set in the style graph.'}),
(walked_path_is_data:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A walked path must be stored as a Path node with ordered STEP edges, as §22 of the graph spec requires.'}),
(record_and_play:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Record must write a path from boxes tapped in order, and Play must step through it.'}),
(styled_paths_and_views:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Styling for a path or a view must be able to change the colour, line and format of chosen things and links, and must be written in the style graph.'}),
(styling_keeps_locked_rules:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Styling must keep the locked rules: every colour that carries meaning appears in a legend, and every colour meets the contrast of the locked style.'}),
(empty_view:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A view that matches nothing must say so and offer to change it; it must never show a blank drawing.'}),
(path_missing_step:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A walked path that steps through something removed must be reported, with the fix to remove the step or restore the thing.'}),
(path_no_steps:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A path with no steps must be reported, never drawn as an empty story.'}),
(steps_numbered:Requirement {section: '7', slice: 2, level: 'should', rule: 'While a path is recorded, each tapped step should show its number.'}),
(plain_english_problems:Requirement {section: '8', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every problem must be stated in plain language, with its place and at least one fix where one exists.'}),
(locked_pickers:Requirement {section: '8', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'In a locked graph, pickers must offer only the kinds and relationships of its schema.'}),
(draft_near_duplicates:Requirement {section: '8', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'In a draft, a new kind or relationship within one or two letters of an existing one must be warned of before it is written.'}),
(offer_lock:Requirement {section: '8', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A draft graph must offer Lock the graph, which writes its schema.'}),
(empty_block_start:Requirement {section: '8', slice: 1, level: 'should', rule: 'An empty block should open on a prompt to start with a thing.'}),
(parked_not_broken:Requirement {section: '8', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'The problems badge must count deferred things apart from problems, and show those past their revisit date as overdue.'}),
(completeness_behind_badge:Requirement {section: '8', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The completeness report of §25 of the graph spec must be shown behind the problems badge: covered, deferred and missing, with a fix for each gap.'}),
(many_problems:Requirement {section: '8', slice: 1, level: 'should', rule: 'When there are more problems than fit a screen, they should be grouped by kind, the worst first.'}),
(not_colour_alone:Requirement {section: '8', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Nothing may be marked by colour or line style alone: estimated and broken links, deferred things, problems and proposed changes each carry an icon and words.'}),
(problems_announced:Requirement {section: '8', slice: 1, level: 'should', rule: 'New problems should be announced to screen readers without moving the focus.'}),
(problem_says_what_to_type:Requirement {section: '8', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Each problem and its fix must say in plain words what to type or change. They must never cite a section of a spec, or the document profile (NEURO-4).'}),
(problem_shown_once:Requirement {section: '8', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A problem must be shown once: in the fix bar while the caret is on its line, else in the list. The suggestions must never cover the fix bar (NEURO-4).'}),
(check_before_write:Requirement {section: '9', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A graphic edit must be checked before it is written, and an edit that adds a problem must ask first.'}),
(no_blind_overwrite:Requirement {section: '9', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'If the block changed since the pane read it, a write must be refused and the pane reloaded.'}),
(block_followed_not_its_copy:Requirement {section: '9', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'When the same block text appears twice in a note, the editor must keep to the block it was opened on and never switch to the copy; if it cannot tell which one that is, it must stop and say so rather than edit or undo either.'}),
(undo_stays_in_its_block:Requirement {section: '9', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Undo must only ever change the block whose edits it is taking back: switching to another block must start that block\'s own history, and must never let Undo write one block\'s text into another.'}),
(undo_never_over_theirs:Requirement {section: '9', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Undo must never undo over a change that another program, a sync or another pane made to the note: after such a change the editor\'s undo history must start again from the note as it now is, and Undo must say why there is nothing to take back.'}),
(nothing_typed_lost:Requirement {section: '9', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Text typed in the pane must never be discarded: a refused write keeps it as a recoverable draft.'}),
(undo_everything:Requirement {section: '9', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every edit must be undoable.'}),
(loud_fallback:Requirement {section: '9', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'If the layout engine cannot load, the generated drawing must be shown with a banner saying it may be stale; a graph must never show as blank.'}),
(plain_gql_kept:Requirement {section: '9', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph block must stay plain GQL that readers without the editor can read.'}),
(host_free_core:Requirement {section: '10', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The core must not import obsidian or vscode.'}),
(format_as_library:Requirement {section: '10', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The reader, the rules, completeness and the layout source must live in their own package, which the plugin uses, and the vault must carry only a generated, read-only copy of each release.'}),
(lsp_shapes:Requirement {section: '10', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Problems, fixes and suggestions must use the shapes of the Language Server Protocol'}),
(one_colour_map:Requirement {section: '10', slice: 1, level: 'should', rule: 'Theme colours should reach each host through one mapping table.'}),
(fifty_in_half_second:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 1, level: 'should', rule: 'A graph of 50 things should be drawn in under half a second on the iPhone.'}),
(large_drawn_quick:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Past 300 things, a graph must be laid out once, in one direction, with Graphviz\'s quick settings, by both renderers alike: a little less tidy, many times faster, so a graph up to the 2,000-thing drawing limit lays out in seconds, not minutes (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(large_drawn_curved:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A graph laid out quickly must keep its links curved, as any other drawing does, never drawn straight from box to box (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(pan_measured_once:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Each step of a zoom or pan must read the size of the drawing\'s viewport once, before it changes the page, so the browser lays the page out at most once a step, however many parts of the card need the size (Roger, 23 Oct 2026: drawing in Obsidian was slow).'}),
(contrast_both_themes:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The drawing must meet the contrast of the locked style in both themes.'}),
(keyboard_reach:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every action must be reachable from the keyboard on desktop.'}),
(keyboard_any_device:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every function must be operable by keyboard alone on any device that has or can have a keyboard, such as a phone or tablet with one attached, in every host and every layout, with nothing that needs a pointer or touch.'}),
(screen_reader_text:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The plain-language reading must be available as a text version of the graph for screen readers.'}),
(wcag_aa:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Every interface of the editor, in every host, must meet WCAG 2.2 at Level AA.'}),
(wcag_new_hosts:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A new host or interface must meet WCAG 2.2 AA before its first release.'}),
(wcag_checked:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Every view and page must pass an automated WCAG 2.2 AA check in the tests, in light and dark themes'}),
(walkthrough_recorded:Requirement {section: '11', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Each stable release must keep a dated record of the accessibility walk-through: every check, in every host and theme, ticked or with its reason. The release and its dry run must fail without one (UX-1).'}),
(layout_byte_identical:Requirement {section: '12', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'For every graph in the vault, the layout source from the core must be byte-identical to that from graph_svg.py.'}),
(round_trips:Requirement {section: '12', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Every graphic action must round-trip: applied and read back it gives the graph intended, and undone it gives the original bytes.'}),
(survives_abuse:Requirement {section: '12', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'No sequence of random edits or random text may crash the viewer or leave it blank.'}),
(tests_first:Requirement {section: '12', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every test must be written before the code it proves, and must be seen to fail before that code is written.'}),
(done_means_proved:Requirement {section: '12', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Every requirement in this spec must be proved by a named test before the editor is called done.'}),
(tests_self_contained:Requirement {section: '12', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Every test, automated or on the device checklist, must contain or generate everything it needs to run; none may depend on a file supplied by hand.'}),
(test_files_generated:Requirement {section: '12', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The images and other files the tests need, including real animated GIF, APNG, WebP and SVG images and a GIF over the size limit, must be made by a script in the repo; a file too large to keep in the repo must be made when the check is set up.'}),
(own_repo:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Source must live in its own Git repository outside the vault, with GitHub as its remote.'}),
(graph_spec_in_repo:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec the code cites must be in the repository, with its section numbers and each rule\'s own words. Every section either library cites must be found in it (ARCH-11).'}),
(vault_stays_clean:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The vault must never be a Git repository or connected to GitHub.'}),
(no_vault_content_in_repo:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Nothing from the vault classified above Public may be committed to the repository.'}),
(no_node_modules_in_vault:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'No node_modules folder may be written into the vault.'}),
(personal_content_refused:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The repository must refuse, before every commit and again in CI, content that looks confidential or personal: vault classification lines, vault note frontmatter, absolute home-folder paths, personal email addresses, phone numbers and card numbers.'}),
(general_patterns_only:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The check must be built from general patterns only, so the repository never holds a list of personal details'}),
(store_files:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The repository must meet the Obsidian community plugin requirements from the first commit: a manifest, versions.json, a README, a licence, and releases that carry main.js, manifest.json and styles.css.'}),
(obsidian_guidelines:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The plugin must follow the Obsidian plugin guidelines and developer policies beyond what the linter checks: sentence case in the interface, styling by CSS classes and the theme variables, every listener and timer registered so that unloading leaves nothing behind, the vault changed only through its own interface, and no use of the global app object.'}),
(one_code_style:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Code must be formatted by a formatter and linted in CI, with TypeScript in strict mode and the Python library checked against PEP 8, so that no layout is a matter of taste.'}),
(naming_conventions:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Names must follow one written convention: files in kebab-case, classes and types in PascalCase, functions and variables in camelCase, constants in UPPER_SNAKE_CASE, Python in snake_case, and each test file named after the file it tests.'}),
(code_layout:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Each package must keep its source in src and its tests in test, with one module for each purpose and the public interface of each package documented where it is declared.'}),
(no_monolith_files:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Code must be split into small modules, each with one responsibility, grouped in folders by feature. No source file may exceed 400 lines, and no test file 600, checked in CI.'}),
(quality_ratchet:Requirement {section: '12', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'CI must measure the code\'s quality metrics against a committed baseline on every release and fail on any that got worse without a recorded reason: file sizes, a dependency vulnerability scan, a secrets scan, bundle size, the work a fixed set of benchmarks takes, counted in CPU instructions and never in elapsed time, flaky tests, the size of a passing quiet test run, and coverage; test duration is reported as a warning only, and a test asserts the work done, never the time taken. A benchmark whose built code, machine, Node and Valgrind are unchanged since its last count may keep that count when a baseline is measured; every release counts every benchmark.'}),
(slice_end_review:Requirement {section: '12', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'The full review of the code quality standard must be run at the end of every slice.'}),
(engine_version_watched:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Every build must read the exact Graphviz version inside the layout engine, name it in the release notes and the notices, and check it against published Graphviz advisories as well as those of the package that wraps it; a known flaw must fail the release.'}),
(layout_contained:Requirement {section: '9', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'The layout engine must run apart from the interface, with a time limit and a size limit, so a huge or hostile graph never freezes Obsidian; a graph over a limit must be shown as its text, with a banner saying why.'}),
(reading_contained:Requirement {section: '9', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Re-reading broken blocks must stop at one budget that every reader shares, so every reader draws the same note the same way.'}),
(unnamed_drawn_suggested:Requirement {section: '4', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A thing written without a name must be drawn under its suggested name, dashed and labelled as suggested, selectable like any box, by every reader alike; nothing is written until its fix is accepted, and the problem stays listed until then.'}),
(requirements_checked_in_ci:Requirement {section: '12', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'CI must check the repository\'s generated copy of these requirements on every change: every requirement of a slice that has started names a test that exists, every test claims a requirement, and no test is marked written while planned or planned while written.'}),
(named_by_feature:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Folders and files must be named for the feature they hold, never utils or misc, so a search by name finds them.'}),
(purpose_line:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every source file must open with one line saying what it does.'}),
(tests_mirror_code:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Each module’s tests must sit in a test file named after it, and that file must run on its own in seconds.'}),
(small_typed_interfaces:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Each module must export a small, typed interface, so that it can be used without reading its inside.'}),
(package_map:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Each package must have a short guide saying where things live, kept up to date in the same change that moves them.'}),
(quiet_test_run:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The tests must have a quiet mode that prints one line when everything passes, and for each failure only its file and line, its name, a short expected-and-actual and the first line of the call stack in the repository\'s own code, with long differences cut short and test logging silenced.'}),
(touched_tests_only:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'It must be possible to run only the tests of the modules a change touches, found by the tests-mirror-the-code naming; the full suite still runs before every merge and in CI.'}),
(ci_runs_what_changed:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A draft pull request\'s CI must run only the tests its change reaches, and its fresh clone only when a setup input changes. A pull request ready for review, main and the release must run everything; the safety checks must always run, and a failed plan must run everything.'}),
(capped_test_run:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every test run started by the repository\'s test commands, in CI as well, must be stopped before it can exhaust the machine: past a memory ceiling for the whole run or a time ceiling, it is killed with all its processes, says in one line which ceiling it hit, how much it measured and how to raise it, and fails; a run within both passes through unchanged.'}),
(ai_session_guide:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The repository must hold a short guide for AI sessions saying how to run the tests cheaply, where things live, and which files never to read.'}),
(generated_out_of_search:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Generated and bulky files, such as the built checker and the lock file, must be kept out of code searches.'}),
(conventions_written:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The conventions must be written once, in a contributing guide in the repository, and checked in CI wherever a tool can check them.'}),
(store_review_rules:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The plugin must pass the Obsidian review rules checked by the eslint-plugin-obsidianmd linter.'}),
(offline_and_mobile_apis:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The plugin must make no network requests at runtime except to an AI the user has set up, and only when the user sends; Import reads a database file on the device and reaches no network (Roger, 20 Oct 2026). The one other request fetches a plug-in file from a link the person typed, once, when they press Add (Roger, 21 Oct 2026)'}),
(no_default_hotkeys:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The plugin must set no default hotkeys.'}),
(community_files:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The repository must hold a code of conduct that says how to report a problem in private, a note that the licence gives no right to the name ThoughtKnot so a fork goes by its own name, and the rule that every commit in a pull request is signed off under the Developer Certificate of Origin (Roger, 18 Oct 2026).'}),
(security_policy:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The repository must hold a security policy that says which versions get fixes, how to report a problem in private through GitHub, never in a public issue, and how soon the reporter hears back (Roger, 18 Oct 2026).'}),
(dco_checked:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every commit from 20 Oct 2026 on that is not a merge or a bot\'s dependency update must carry a sign-off naming a person, never a bot or an AI; a commit any AI agent writes (Claude, Antigravity, graph-note) carries Roger\'s sign-off, as he directs and certifies that work, and CI refuses one without it, or one signed off only by an agent\'s name (Roger, 19 Oct 2026; every agent, 20 Oct 2026).'}),
(version_promise_told:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The README and the For developers wiki page must state the version rule before 1.0.0, that a step in the leftmost part that is not 0 may break the library\'s interface, and each release\'s notes must hold a Library changes section that says it; the release must fail without it, and the release check must try it first (Roger, 19 Oct 2026).'}),
(agents_pr_rules:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'AGENTS.md must hold a short list for AI sessions to read before they open a pull request: merge commits only, every command that rewrites a file CI checks, the sign-off rule, and who approves a pull request and how (Roger, 19 Oct 2026).'}),
(pr_description_checked:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A pull request from an AI agent\'s branch (claude/ or antigravity/) must carry a Requirements: line naming requirements the spec holds, that agent\'s label, and no private session link; when it changes a test, its tests-first records must show a test failing on the old code, or a Tests first: line must say why none can. The pull request template asks for each, and CI fails a pull request missing one (Eve, the working group\'s advisor, through Roger, 21 Oct 2026).'}),
(no_private_links:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'No commit from 21 Oct 2026 on may carry a private session link, such as a Claude-Session: line or a link to a Claude session; CI refuses one (Roger, 21 Oct 2026).'}),
(agents_read_asks:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'AGENTS.md must tell every AI session to start by reading its own "To [name]" asks in the working group discussion, #825, and to do them before new work or say why not (Roger, 21 Oct 2026; issue #846).'}),
(roles_from_to:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'docs/agents/ROLES.md must say that every post opens with "From [writer]:" and every request with "To [name]:", with no signature at the bottom (Roger, 21 Oct 2026).'}),
(working_group_reply:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Sara (Claude) must be able to answer in the working group discussion, #825, from any session, without Roger pasting it: a workflow she starts posts her reply, after the same checks as the watcher\'s replies (Roger, 21 Oct 2026).'}),
(help_route_public:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The home page and the wiki\'s Home page must say where anyone, not only a collaborator, can ask for help or report a problem: GitHub Discussions, which the settings keep open; neither may promise issues that only collaborators can open (Roger, 19 Oct 2026).'}),
(contribution_by_invitation:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The contributing guide must say at its top that only collaborators may open pull requests and issues until the community release, that ideas and questions go to GitHub Discussions, and how to ask to join; no document may say anyone can send a pull request while the settings keep pull requests to collaborators (Roger, 19 Oct 2026).'}),
(mpl_licence:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The plugin and both libraries must be released under the Mozilla Public License 2.0, so anyone may fork, change and contribute, and changed files stay open (Roger, 18 Oct 2026; MIT before).'}),
(licence_allow_list:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every library bundled into a release must carry a licence on an allow-list compatible with MIT distribution, checked in CI; any other licence fails the build.'}),
(licence_inside_package:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The check must read the licences of what is compiled inside a package, not only the licence the package declares.'}),
(notices_shipped:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every bundled library must be named in a third-party notices file shipped with each release, with its licence text, and with where its source can be obtained when its licence asks for that.'}),
(build_tool_licences:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'should', rule: 'Build tools that are not shipped should still carry a licence on the allow-list; a GPL tool is allowed only where it runs in CI alone and is never shipped.'}),
(one_runtime_dependency:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Runtime dependencies must be kept to the Graphviz layout engine and the SQLite engine of Import (§20, sql.js), both WebAssembly held inside the bundle and never fetched, except that the browser host may add one Markdown renderer (§26)'}),
(exact_pins:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every dependency must be pinned to an exact version, with the lockfile committed, and installed with npm ci.'}),
(no_install_scripts:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Install scripts of dependencies must never run.'}),
(signatures_verified:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Registry signatures must be verified in CI before every build.'}),
(seven_day_cooling_off:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A new version of a dependency must wait at least seven days before it is adopted, except for a security fix.'}),
(security_fixes_flow:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Security fixes must be raised automatically as soon as they are published, as pull requests that run the full test suite.'}),
(actions_pinned:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every GitHub Action must be pinned to a full commit hash.'}),
(least_privilege_ci:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'CI must run with read-only permissions, except the release job.'}),
(built_by_ci:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every release must be built by CI from a tag, with a build provenance attestation once the repository is public, never from a local machine.'}),
(release_steps_written:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'RELEASING.md must hold the steps of a release in order, how a bad one is followed, and every key a successor needs with where it is made; one command must bump every file that carries the version, and a draft release left more than a day must fail the nightly (risks OPS-3, OPS-1).'}),
(release_from_main:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A stable release must be built only from a tag on a commit main holds, and the tag must match the manifest version; both are checked first, before anything is installed, so a wrong tag fails in seconds (risk OPS-2).'}),
(ci_jobs_time_limited:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'Every job of every workflow must set a time limit, about twice its usual length, so a hung step never holds a runner for hours (risk OPS-4).'}),
(guards_never_run:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A guard in a note must be read as comparisons joined by and, or and not, in every library and reader, and never run as code; a guard holding anything else must be refused (risk SEC-1).'}),
(node_test_runner:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'should', rule: 'Tests should use the test runner built into Node, so no test framework is a dependency.'}),
(installed_as_files:Requirement {section: '13', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The plugin must be installed as real files in .obsidian/plugins/thoughtknot/, so Obsidian Sync carries it to the phone.'}),
(properties_switch:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Every property of things and of links must be able to be switched on or off for showing inline in the drawing.'}),
(properties_remembered:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The choice of properties shown must be saved in the style graph, for the whole graph or for one view.'}),
(properties_readable:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Properties shown inline must be drawn at the size and contrast of the locked style, below the text of their box or link.'}),
(inspector:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'An inspector must show every property of the selected thing or link, and allow each to be edited, added or removed.'}),
(inspector_in_details:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'When the drawing’s card is in edit mode, the details shown on tapping a box or a link must be the inspector, editable in place.'}),
(inspector_placement:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'On a phone the inspector must open as a bottom sheet; on desktop as a panel that can stay open while things are selected in turn.'}),
(inspector_native:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The inspector must look and behave like the Properties panel of Obsidian itself: one row for each property with its name and a field suited to its type, a date picker for a date and a switch for true or false, adding and removing in place, in the spacing, type and colours of the theme.'}),
(list_as_linked_things:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 2, level: 'should', rule: 'Choosing List for a property should hold its values as things linked to this one, one thing for each value, of a kind and by a relationship the user names, reusing a thing that already has that name; the inspector shows them as chips, added and removed in place.'}),
(inspector_names:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'The inspector must offer property names already used on things of the same kind.'}),
(typed_values:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'A value must be checked against its type before it is written: text, number, date, date and time, or true or false.'}),
(inspector_locked:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 1, level: 'must', rule: 'In a locked graph, the inspector must offer only the properties its schema declares for the kind.'}),
(inspector_locked_types:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 2, level: 'should', rule: 'A property’s ElementType should be able to carry a datatype — text, number, date, date and time, or true or false — written by Lock from the values in use, and the inspector should then offer that type.'}),
(crowded_properties:Requirement {section: '14', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'When the properties switched on would crowd a box, the drawing should say how many are hidden rather than overlap them.'}),
(diagram_type_set:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must offer these diagram types: network, tree, flow, radial, lanes, timeline and table.'}),
(type_only_when_fits:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A diagram type must be offered only when the data fits it, and a type that does not fit must say why.'}),
(tree_fits:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A tree must be offered only when every thing has at most one parent along the chosen relationship, and no relationship loops back.'}),
(timeline_fits:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A timeline must be offered for every graph with things in it, dated or not. Things without the value it is ordered by must stand together after the rest, and never stop it being drawn.'}),
(timeline_order:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A timeline must be able to be ordered by date, by a number such as a step, by the order things are written, or by their links, each thing after the things that link to it. With no order chosen, it must be ordered by date when a thing carries one, else by links when there are any, else as written. The order must be saved in the style graph.'}),
(timeline_show:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A timeline must be able to show every thing, only the things with a date, or only the things without one. The choice must be saved in the style graph.'}),
(timeline_choice_card:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A card drawn as a timeline must offer its order and what it shows. In Reading view a choice must only redraw the card, said not saved; where the note is edited it must be saved in the style graph.'}),
(suggest_type:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must suggest the type that best fits the data, and must never switch type without being asked.'}),
(type_remembered:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The diagram type chosen must be saved in the style graph, for the whole graph or for one view.'}),
(type_try_reading:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'In Reading view the diagram types must still be offered: one chosen there is drawn at once but never saved, is said to be not saved, and can be put back to the saved type; where the note is edited, a type so looked at must be offered to keep, which saves it.'}),
(type_keeps_place:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Changing the diagram type, saved or only looked at, must keep the card as it was: full screen stays full screen, the thing selected stays selected with its details open, and the zoom is kept, the selected thing brought into view where the layout moved it.'}),
(arrows_switchable:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The arrowheads on links must be able to be hidden or shown for the whole graph, for one view, or for one relationship type, and the choice must be saved in the style graph, never in the data.'}),
(arrows_shown_unless_hidden:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Arrowheads must be drawn unless the style graph hides them, so a drawing never changes how it reads without being asked.'}),
(direction_never_lost:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'With arrowheads hidden, the direction of a link must still be given by its reading when tapped and in the text version of the graph.'}),
(arrows_both_renderers:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The Python drawing script must hide and show arrowheads the same way.'}),
(type_one_look:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Every diagram type must keep the locked style: its colours, type sizes and contrast.'}),
(type_one_way_to_work:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Tapping, the inspector, editing and zoom must work the same way in every diagram type.'}),
(type_both_renderers:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Every diagram type drawn by Graphviz must have the same layout source in the core as in graph_svg.py.'}),
(plugin_diagram_type_named:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A style graph may choose a diagram type a plug-in adds, by the plug-in\'s id, a dot and the type\'s word, such as acme.charts.sankey. Where no plug-in draws it, the graph must be drawn as a network, saying which plug-in draws it, alike in the core and in graph_svg.py.'}),
(type_stops_fitting:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'When an edit makes the data stop fitting the chosen diagram type, such as a second parent in a tree, the editor must say so and offer the nearest type that fits; it must never draw the graph wrongly.'}),
(large_way_in:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'On a graph of more than 1,000 things, the editor should offer a view or the table as the way in, beside the drawing. A smaller graph is drawn with no such note (Roger, 22 Oct 2026: 50 was far too low).'}),
(wide_types_phone:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'On a phone, a wide tree, flow or timeline should scroll in one direction only, with the other fitted.'}),
(radial_centre_chosen:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A radial drawing must be laid out around the thing its style graph names as its centre, the others in rings by how many links away they are; with none named, or the one named not in the drawing, around the thing with the most links. A centre named for another type, or for a thing the graph does not have, must be a problem with its fix. The same in the core and in graph_svg.py.'}),
(radial_centre_pick:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'In a radial drawing, a thing’s details must offer Put in the centre, and C on its box must do the same: the drawing is laid out again around it, each box gliding from where it was, at once when the system asks for reduced motion. Where the note is edited, the centre must be saved in the style graph; in Reading view it must only be looked at, never saved, as a diagram type is. While a centre is picked, the card must say so and offer Back to the usual centre.'}),
(radial_links_round:Requirement {section: '15', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A radial drawing must curve each link around the things it does not join, never through or over them, unless the style graph routes links straight or at right angles.'}),
(export_two_scopes:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'An Export button must export either the current view or the whole diagram.'}),
(export_formats:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Images must be offered as SVG and PNG.'}),
(export_data_formats:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The data must be offered as a GQL block of the things exported, as CSV, and as GraphML.'}),
(view_export_stays_view:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'A view export must contain only the things in the view, unless the faded context is asked for.'}),
(export_self_explaining:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'An exported image must carry its title, its legend, and the note and date it came from.'}),
(export_fixed_theme:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'An exported image must have its theme colours fixed, light or dark as chosen, so it reads the same outside Obsidian.'}),
(export_png_sharp:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'An exported PNG must be drawn at no less than twice natural size, so its text stays sharp.'}),
(export_share_sheet:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'On a phone, export must open the share sheet.'}),
(export_companion_note:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'An export saved into the vault can get a companion note, as configured.'}),
(export_lossless:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Data exported must read back into a graph identical to the things and links exported.'}),
(empty_export:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Exporting an empty graph or view must say there is nothing to export, and write no file.'}),
(failed_export:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'If an export cannot be written, the editor must say why and leave no half-written file.'}),
(big_export:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'An export too large for one sharp image should be split across pages.'}),
(classified_export:Requirement {section: '16', slice: 2, level: 'must', rule: 'Exporting a note whose classification is above a configured level must name its classification and ask before the file is written.'}),
(proposal_shown_not_applied:Requirement {section: '17', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A change set proposed by an AI must be shown in the editor over the graph, with additions, changes and removals each marked, and nothing written.'}),
(proposal_one_by_one:Requirement {section: '17', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Each change in a proposal must be accepted or rejected on its own, or all at once.'}),
(accepting_is_an_edit:Requirement {section: '17', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Accepting a change must write it as text, checked first, like any other edit.'}),
(rejecting_recorded:Requirement {section: '17', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Rejecting a change must leave the graph unchanged and record the rejection as a decision in a judgement-graph block (§23 of the graph spec) in the note being edited, reusing one if the note has it.'}),
(proposal_provenance:Requirement {section: '17', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A proposal must show who proposed it, when, and what it read; a proposal whose basis has moved since must say so before anything is accepted.'}),
(unclassified_strictest:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A note with no classification must count as the most sensitive level, so it goes only to a model cleared for everything, unless the user sets a different default for unlabelled notes in the settings.'}),
(proposals_checked:Requirement {section: '17', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must run the graph spec\'s own change-set check on every proposal and show its result, and every proposal it writes, removals and decisions included, must pass that check.'}),
(decider_named:Requirement {section: '17', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'The person who accepts or rejects a proposal should be recorded by the name set in the settings, or as "you" when none is set.'}),
(person_decides:Requirement {section: '17', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must never apply a proposal without a person accepting it.'}),
(big_proposal:Requirement {section: '17', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'A proposal with many changes should be reviewable in groups, with accept-all applying to one group at a time.'}),
```
