(proposals_phone:Requirement {section: '17', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'On a phone, the changes of a proposal must be listed in the bottom sheet, one per row, each with accept and reject.'}),
(proposals_read_back:Requirement {section: '17', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'Each proposed change should be read back in plain language, as lines are in the text pane.'}),
(cc_editor_opens:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: '`/graph edit <note>` must open the note in Claude Code\'s browser pane with the drawing, details and edit pane of the Obsidian host.'}),
(cc_core_only:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The browser editor must take every reading, drawing, check and edit from the core and a display layer shared with the Obsidian host, and import no Obsidian code or stand-in for it.'}),
(cc_same_bytes:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'An edit made in the browser editor must write the same bytes as the same edit in Obsidian, proved by shared cases.'}),
(cc_start_failure:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'If the server cannot start, the pane must say why and still show the drawing `/graph` gives.'}),
(cc_loopback_only:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor\'s server must listen on 127.0.0.1 only, on a port the system chooses, and must not be reachable from any other machine, Tailscale included; remote reach would need its own security review first.'}),
(cc_one_server:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Each Claude Code session must start its own server, and no two sessions may share one.'}),
(cc_session_token:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Every API call must carry a secret token made fresh for each server start and compared in constant time; without it the call is refused and nothing about the note is said. Only the page itself may be served without the token, and it must hold no note data.'}),
(cc_token_unkept:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The token must reach the page only in the address\'s fragment, be removed from the address once read, and never be written to disk, a log, page storage or the conversation.'}),
(cc_host_checked:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A request naming any Host but the server\'s own loopback name and port must be refused.'}),
(cc_origin_checked:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A request that changes anything must come from the server\'s own origin, and the server must answer no cross-origin request.'}),
(cc_csp:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The page must run under a content security policy that allows only its own origin, no inline script and no eval.'}),
(cc_body_limit:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The server must refuse a request body larger than the core\'s read limit for a note.'}),
(cc_no_remote:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must load nothing from outside its server, and the server must make no outgoing request but one: the read-only GitHub reads the user asks for to review a change, made by the user\'s own GitHub command line (github_review_items).'}),
(cc_server_stops:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The server must stop when its session ends, when the process that started it exits, after an idle period with no page open, and at once on `/graph stop`.'}),
(cc_threats_tested:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Each threat in the security model must be proved by a test against the running server: wrong Host, wrong Origin, no token, path or symlink escape, stale write, oversized body and the content security policy.'}),
(cc_open_notes_only:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The server must read and write only the notes opened for it in this session, by an id it gave, never by a path from the page.'}),
(cc_no_escape:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A note whose real path leaves the vault, is not a .md file, or lies in a hidden folder must be refused.'}),
(cc_atomic_write:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Every write must go to a temporary file beside the note and be renamed into place.'}),
(cc_write_if_unchanged:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A write must name the version of the note it was made against, and be refused, with what was typed kept as a draft, when the note has changed since.'}),
(cc_redrawn_on_change:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'When the open note changes on disk, the editor must draw it again within a second, keeping what is typed or saying there is a conflict.'}),
(cc_drafts_outside:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Drafts must be kept outside the vault, in the mod\'s own data folder, by note, and offered again when the note is next opened.'}),
(cc_undo_safe:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Undo must never undo over a change another program wrote; it must say so instead.'}),
(cc_exports_new_only:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'An export must be written only as new files in the export folder, never over an existing file.'}),
(cc_export_same:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Export must offer the formats and the classification question of §16, with PNG drawn by the page.'}),
(cc_settings_shared:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'should', rule: 'The browser editor should use the vault\'s ThoughtKnot settings, read only.'}),
(cc_classified_asks:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A note classified above the level set for Claude must open in the pane only after the user confirms, since Claude can read the pane.'}),
(cc_credential_never:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A note at the highest classification must never open in the pane, whatever the settings.'}),
(cc_transcript_clean:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The mod must put neither the token nor the note\'s text into the conversation.'}),
(cc_text_not_html:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Note text, names and properties must be shown as text, and Markdown notes rendered with raw HTML turned off.'}),
(cc_claude_only:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Claude must be the only AI the browser editor offers.'}),
(cc_claude_proposes:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A change Claude makes to a note open in the pane must reach it through the mod as a proposal, shown as §17 requires and written only when the user accepts it, never through the server\'s write. Claude\'s own Write, Edit, NotebookEdit and Bash calls that reach that note must be refused, and the refusal must point to the propose tool.'}),
(cc_one_renderer:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The browser host may add one small, permissively licensed Markdown renderer, with raw HTML turned off, and nothing else; its icons must ship as image files, never as a code library.'}),
(cc_worker_same_origin:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Layout must run in a worker loaded from the server\'s own origin, under the core\'s limits.'}),
(cc_theme_follows:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The page must follow the system\'s light or dark scheme with the core\'s palette, at the locked contrast.'}),
(cc_icons:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'should', rule: 'Icons should be bundled with the editor and never shown as their names.'}),
(cc_keyboard:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Every action must be reachable from the keyboard, and every control must have an accessible name.'}),
(cc_reduced_motion:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'Every animation must be instant or still when the system asks for reduced motion.'}),
(cc_text_version:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The plain-language text version of the graph must be in the page for screen readers.'}),
(cc_offline:Requirement {section: '26', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The browser editor must work with no network connection.'}),
(links_shown:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Every link in a graph must be shown as a link wherever it appears: in a box, on a card, in the inspector and in the text pane.'}),
(host_opens_links:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Opening a link must use the host\'s own handler: in Obsidian its link opener, so a modifier-click opens in a new tab or pane as anywhere else in Obsidian; in VS Code its open command.'}),
(attachments_at_a_glance:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A thing with attachments must show a paperclip with their count, and the card and the inspector must list them by name and type.'}),
(link_preview:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'On desktop, hovering a link must show the host\'s own preview of the note or attachment; on a phone, a long press must.'}),
(back_where_you_were:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'After following a link from the editor, the host\'s back step must return to the graph as it was left: the same view, zoom and selection.'}),
(broken_links_problems:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A link that names nothing must be drawn as broken, and reported with a fix: create the note, or pick the file it most likely meant.'}),
(renames_follow:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'When a note or attachment is renamed in the host, every link to it inside graph blocks must be updated in the same step, or reported as broken.'}),
(safe_to_click:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Only links to notes and files in the vault, and web and mail addresses, may be opened; any other kind of link must be shown as text and never run.'}),
(links_by_keyboard:Requirement {section: '18', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Every link must be reachable and openable from the keyboard on desktop.'}),
(ai_off_until_chosen:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'AI features must be off until the user turns them on and sets up where requests go.'}),
(host_ai_first:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Where the host offers its own interface to the AI models the user already uses, such as the language model API of VS Code, the editor must offer it, so the choices and consents the user already made there apply.'}),
(user_models:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must be able to set up models of their own, local or remote, each by its address.'}),
(models_by_purpose:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must be able to set up several models and say which is used for which purpose, such as research questions, proposing changes or restricted processing.'}),
(keys_kept_secret:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A key for an AI service must be kept in the secret store of the host where it has one, and never in the vault or in any settings file that syncs.'}),
(model_ceiling:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Each model must carry the highest classification of content it may receive, set by the user; where the vault keeps a destination matrix, it is the default.'}),
(never_above_ceiling:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Content above the ceiling of a model must never be sent to it; the editor must say why, and offer a model cleared for it if one is set up.'}),
(never_credential:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Content at the highest configured classification must never be sent to any model.'}),
(user_chooses_what:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must choose what is sent: the whole graph, a view, or the things selected, and nothing else from the vault.'}),
(seen_before_sent:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Before anything is sent, the user must see exactly what will be sent and to which model, and confirm it.'}),
(never_in_background:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Nothing may be sent to an AI except when the user sends it.'}),
(answers_are_proposals:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'An answer must come back as a proposal, shown and accepted or rejected as §17 says; an AI never writes to the graph. What is sent from the note must be fenced and named as data, never instructions, so text in a note cannot steer the model.'}),
(record_every_send:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Every send must be recorded: what was sent, to which model, and when, so research can be traced back to what the model was shown.'}),
(ai_unreachable:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'If a model cannot be reached or refuses, the editor must say so plainly and change nothing.'}),
(ai_disclosed:Requirement {section: '19', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The README must say that the plugin sends content only to models the user sets up, and only when the user sends it. It must say that a model at a remote address keeps what it receives under its provider\'s terms and retention, and that a model at localhost keeps it on the device.'}),
(zoom_out_to_fit:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Zooming out must stop where the whole drawing fits the view, or at natural size when the whole drawing fits at a larger size; from slice 3 this replaces the lower limit of a quarter in §3, and the upper limit of four times stays.'}),
(gestures_stay_in_drawing:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'On a phone, a touch the drawing uses (one finger panning a drawing zoomed in or in full screen, a pinch, or a finger moving a box) must not reach the host, so it never slides Obsidian\'s side panels in or moves the window; a swipe on a drawing that is only fitted in a note must still reach it.'}),
(card_keeps_focus_from_editor:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'In Live Preview, pressing a button, a link or the drawing in a card must give it the focus without scrolling and never give the focus to the editor around the card, so the page never jumps to the editor\'s cursor; a press in a text box in the card still places its cursor there.'}),
(zoom_fit_recomputed:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The zoom-out limit must be worked out again whenever the view is resized, full-screen is entered or left, or the graph changes.'}),
(focus_neighbours:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Selecting a thing must keep it, its links and the things at their other ends at full strength, and fade everything else, without moving or hiding anything.'}),
(focus_link:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Selecting a link must keep it and the two things it joins at full strength, and fade everything else.'}),
(focus_readable:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Faded things must stay readable at the contrast §11 requires, and focus must be shown by strength and outline, never by colour alone.'}),
(focus_cleared:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Tapping empty space or pressing Escape must clear the selection and the focus.'}),
(focus_depth:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'A setting should choose how far focus reaches: one step, the default, or two.'}),
(hover_highlight:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Pointing at a thing or a link must highlight it, and for a link the two things it joins, for as long as the pointer stays, on top of any focus.'}),
(hover_not_alone:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The same highlight must follow keyboard focus, and on touch the tap that selects gives it, so that nothing depends on hover (§5).'}),
(hover_still:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A highlight must never move, resize or re-lay the drawing.'}),
(details_links_listed:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The details pane and the inspector must list a thing\'s links grouped by relationship type and direction, read in words, with a count for each group of more than one — *reports to Ann*, *is reported to by Bob and Cy (2)*.'}),
(details_keep_view:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Opening and closing a thing\'s details must keep the drawing\'s zoom and place, in full screen and the pane too, where the details take room from the drawing. In full screen the details must take no more than part of it and scroll, so the drawing stays in sight.'}),
(details_choose_moves:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Choosing a link or a linked thing in that list must make it the selection, bring it into view and show its details, keeping the zoom unless the thing would be too small to read.'}),
(details_said:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A thing\'s details must open with a sentence or two in plain words saying what is going on: what kind of thing it is, what links to it and what it links to, naming up to two things in a group and counting more, and how many properties and problems it has.'}),
(details_ins_outs:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'A thing\'s links must be shown in two labelled parts, links in before links out, each with its count and saying so when it has none, each link on a single line of its own, its reading and then the thing at its other end, a group of more than one counted on its first line; side by side where the panel is wide, one above the other where it is narrow.'}),
(details_properties_table:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 4, level: 'must', rule: 'The properties a thing\'s or link\'s details show to be read must be a table of name and value, read by a screen reader as name and value pairs.'}),
(details_move_motion:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The move to a chosen thing must be briefly animated, and instant when the system asks for reduced motion.'}),
(details_trail:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'The pane should keep a trail of the things chosen this way, with back and forward steps, so a walk through the graph can be retraced.'}),
(selection_pointer:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'When the selection is out of view, an arrow at the edge of the view should point to it and bring it into view when tapped.'}),
(details_on_screen:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'In view mode, choosing a thing or a link in a card must show its details within the visible part of the screen, without the user scrolling and without covering the thing chosen.'}),
(sheet_pinned:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'In view mode, a card\'s details must open as a sheet pinned to the bottom of the visible screen, over the card, staying in place while the note scrolls, until it is closed or the card leaves the screen.'}),
(sheet_keeps_thing_clear:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'When the sheet opens or changes size, the drawing must pan so the chosen thing stays visible above it.'}),
(sheet_min_max:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A toggle in the sheet\'s header must switch it between minimised, a single bar showing the chosen thing\'s name and kind, and restored, a panel showing the full details, scrolling within itself, sized so the chosen thing and some of the drawing stay visible above it; it never fills the screen.'}),
(sheet_state_remembered:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'The sheet should open in the state last chosen, for the rest of the session.'}),
(sheet_closed:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A close button, Escape, or clearing the selection must close the sheet.'}),
(sheet_reachable:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The toggle and the close button must have accessible names, work by keyboard, and be at least 44 points on touch.'}),
(sheet_docked:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'A button in the sheet\'s header must choose where the sheet sits over the note\'s visible part: its bottom, the default, its top, or either side; the chosen thing stays clear of it wherever it sits, and every sheet opened after opens there, for the rest of the session.'}),
(sheet_resized:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The edge of the sheet facing the drawing must be a resize bar, dragged with a pointer or moved with the arrow keys once focused, Home and End taking it to its least and most; it is never smaller than the sheet\'s bar, never so big that the chosen thing and some of the drawing cannot be seen beside it. The sheet keeps the size and place set whatever its details hold, never growing or shrinking to fit them, and they hold for the rest of the session.'}),
(sheet_layout_kept:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'Where the host keeps data of its own, the sheet\'s place, size and state should be kept there, so the details open as they were left after the host restarts.'}),
(details_scroll:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Wherever a card\'s details are shown, as the sheet at any place and size, in the card, or in a side pane, details that do not fit must scroll within that space, the header with its buttons staying in sight.'}),
(details_in_host_pane:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'In a host with side panes of its own, such as Obsidian\'s sidebars, the sheet\'s place button should also offer such a pane: the details then open there, to read and, while the block is edited, to change, following whatever is chosen in the card; closing the pane closes the details, and choosing a place on the screen brings them back over the card.'}),
(note_open_beside:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'A thing that stands for a note should offer, in its details, to open that note beside the graph in a pane of the host\'s own, to read and edit there.'}),
(untangle_neighbourhood:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'A thing\'s details should offer *Show only this and its neighbours*, filtering the drawing to the thing and what lies within one or two steps, with a banner naming the filter and a step back to the whole graph.'}),
(untangle_toggle:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'The *Show only this and its neighbours* button should be a toggle: while its thing is shown only, it should say *Show the whole graph*, and pressing it should show the whole graph again.'}),
(untangle_regroup:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'Showing only a thing and its neighbours should lay them out again on their own, so they sit together, fitted to the card, with each box gliding to its new place unless the system asks for reduced motion; showing the whole graph again should bring back its own layout and the zoom it had before.'}),
(untangle_legend:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'Choosing a kind or a relationship type in the legend should bring it forward and fade the rest, and choosing it again should hide it, saved only when the user saves the view (§7).'}),
(untangle_path:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'With two things selected, the editor should offer to bring forward the shortest path between them, or say that there is none.'}),
(untangle_labels:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'Link labels should show only when zoomed in far enough to read them, and always on links in focus or highlighted.'}),
(untangle_hubs:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'A thing with more links than a set number should be able to draw them folded into one stub marked with their count, opened from the stub or from its details.'}),
(untangle_groups:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'A group the drawing shows (its group nudges) should be able to fold into one box showing its count, and open again.'}),
(untangle_merge:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'The style graph should be able to merge links running between the same groups the drawing shows into shared trunks, each link still tappable and read as before.'}),
(untangle_routing:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'The style graph should offer straight, curved or right-angled links, so a dense graph can trade curves for fewer crossings.'}),
(untangle_find:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'A search box should find things by name or property and, on choosing one, select it and bring it into view as the details pane does.'}),
(untangle_keyboard:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'should', rule: 'With a thing selected, the arrow keys should move the selection along its links to the next thing, so a graph can be walked without a pointer.'}),
(untangle_data_untouched:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Focusing, filtering, folding and merging must change only the drawing, never the data.'}),
(view_switches:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Four of these change the drawing before anyone asks; each of the four that is built must have an on and off switch in a *View* menu on the graph\'s bar: labels by zoom, busy hubs folded, groups folded and parallel links merged.'}),
(view_switch_defaults:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'The plugin\'s settings must hold the default for each of the four switches, used by every graph until its *View* menu changes it: labels by zoom on, busy hubs folded on, groups folded off, parallel links merged off.'}),
(view_switch_view_only:Requirement {section: '25', slice: 3, level: 'must', rule: 'Turning a switch must redraw the graph at once, keep the selection and focus, and be saved with the graph only when the user saves the view (§7).'}),
(type_instance_split:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph mesh must model lasting type nodes and separate instance nodes, each linked to its type, so that a query can ask about the type in general or about one instance.'}),
(instance_inherits_type:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'An instance must inherit every property from its type by default and must be able to override any property locally, without changing the type or any other instance.'}),
(type_suggestion_bundle:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'When instance evidence for a change to its type is strong enough, the change must be offered, never applied by itself: it is written as a labelled bundle in a review lens, a person applies it in one step, it rolls back in one step and it is recorded in provenance. A change to a permission-bearing type or to a historical item must always be marked for a closer look (Roger, 20 Oct 2026: a person always applies an AI change).'}),
(format_version_named:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'A block must be able to name the format it is written in on its graph line, and a reader must refuse a newer major format plainly with one problem code from both libraries, keeping the text as written, so an older app never takes a newer block for a broken one (risk ARCH-4, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(schema_version_moves:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'A graph must keep the schema version it names, such as decision@1, until its graph line is changed. A move to decision@2 must be offered as a change a person applies, saying what the new version adds or drops, never made silently (risk ARCH-4, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(rename_follows_notes:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'When a thing is renamed, every block of its graph that names it, in every note, must be rewritten as one bundle a person applies, and a note that cannot be rewritten must be listed with why, never left to disagree unseen (risk NEURO-10, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(same_as_across_graphs:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'Inside a graph, in every note that holds a block of it, a thing\'s name must be its id. Across graphs, two things must be one only when a written SAME_AS link to a Ref says so; things of the same name in two graphs with no such link must stay two and be listed as a guess, and a SAME_AS link whose Ref names nothing must be listed, never joined (risk ARCH-6, Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(mesh_one_format:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph mesh must hold data, queries and structure in one graph format, written as GQL, so that one engine reads, checks and queries all three.'}),
(mesh_text_canonical:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'Each note\'s text must stay the canonical, human-readable store, each note holding its own fragment of graph statements, inserts and any schema it needs, so that deleting every derived store loses nothing authored.'}),
(standard_schemas:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The library must ship standard schemas, each a shared schema named with its version (decision@1 first), so that a graph whose graph line names one is typed by it with no schema in its note or its vault; a vault schema of the same name and version must be used in its place, and a standard schema must never be copied into a note.'}),
(schema_named_found:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A graph whose graph line names a shared schema that neither the vault nor the standard schemas hold must be warned of on its graph line, naming the schema, saying its types are not checked, and offering each version of that schema there is as the fix; a host that cannot see the vault must not warn.'}),
(mesh_index_rebuilds:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'The engine must watch the notes, read them into the graph and keep a derived, queryable store, such as SQLite, that is always rebuildable, and moving to another backend must be a rebuild from the text, never a rewrite.'}),
(mesh_work_in_engine:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'Pathfinding, centroid recomputation, altitude projection and process transitions must run in the engine or the derived store, never in the note layer.'}),
(mesh_engine_parity:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'The JavaScript and Python engines must give identical results for the same query on the same data, joins included, proved by shared parity cases.'}),
(mesh_surfaces_share_data:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'Obsidian, Visual Studio Code and the web view must all read the same underlying data, so an edit made in one shows in the others.'}),
(mesh_rebuilt_alike:Requirement {section: '27', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'Every derived store, whether an index, a view, a current-state link or a centroid, must rebuild from the canonical text with identical results.'}),
(conflicts_flagged_kept:Requirement {section: '28', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'A conflict found when graphs are joined must be flagged and made findable, with both sides kept, and it must never block the join or overwrite either side silently.'}),
(certainty_in_words:Requirement {section: '28', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'Every claim must carry its certainty as a likelihood word with its range and a confidence counted from its evidence, as in §20 of the graph spec, never as a typed number, and two claims that disagree must become contested, with neither asserted, until the dispute is settled.'}),
(certainty_query_dimension:Requirement {section: '28', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'Queries must be able to filter and rank on certainty and deliberately find the shaky claims, and any number used for ranking must be a sort key derived from the likelihood word and the counted confidence, never stored or typed.'}),
(doubt_stays_local:Requirement {section: '28', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'A dispute must lower the standing of the disputed claim only, and must not spread across its cluster unless a view or a parent cluster asks for it.'}),
(contested_link_marker:Requirement {section: '28', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'A link between a well-supported thing and a contested one must carry a contested-link marker, and the well-supported thing\'s certainty must not change because of it.'}),
(hand_clusters_contestable:Requirement {section: '28', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'A cluster the user made by hand must start as asserted but must still become contested when a join disagrees with it, like any other claim.'}),
(history_before_join:Requirement {section: '29', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph mesh must keep versioned history and provenance, so the user can see the graph as it was before a join and ask when a claim became contested.'}),
(two_kinds_of_time:Requirement {section: '29', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'Recorded time and real-world time must both be kept and indexed as query filters, and altitude and lenses must work as at any date on either axis.'}),
(changes_logged_with_provenance:Requirement {section: '29', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'Every change, every AI query and every automatically applied bundle must be logged with its provenance.'}),
(mesh_join_reports_gaps:Requirement {section: '30', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'Several graphs must be able to join, overlap and interact, and the join must keep both sets and report the contradictions and gaps it finds.'}),
(same_join_every_author:Requirement {section: '30', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'The same join rules must apply whether the author edits on a desktop, on a phone or is an AI writing graph statements, so identical edits give identical results apart from who made them, and a merge at the level of the text must never stand in for a decision about meaning.'}),
(state_names_start_doubtful:Requirement {section: '30', slice: 6, level: 'must', rule: 'When two joined process machines have states with the same name, the match must start as a guess, never an assertion, until the user confirms the two are in the same domain.'}),
(recursive_centroids:Requirement {section: '31', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Each level\'s centroid must be a new node that can join a cluster above it, in the cycle cluster, centroid, promote, repeat, with no special handling for any domain.'}),
(stored_centroids:Requirement {section: '31', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'A centroid must be a stored node whose id is derived from its cluster\'s identity, so it survives a rebuild, and it must be recomputed only when a member joins or leaves one of the clusters it stands for.'}),
(centroid_cohesion:Requirement {section: '31', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'A centroid\'s certainty must reflect how tightly its cluster holds together, both how interconnected it is and how well supported its members are, so that a high view cannot hide friction.'}),
(centroid_cycle_guard:Requirement {section: '31', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Recomputing centroids must update each one at most once per change and repeat until values stop moving, below a small threshold and within a maximum number of passes, and if the maximum is reached the cluster must be flagged unsettled rather than returned as if correct.'}),
(cluster_kind_recorded:Requirement {section: '31', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'A cluster must be either found by an algorithm, carrying a counted confidence, or made by hand and asserted, and every membership must record which kind it is so that views can filter on it.'}),
(multiple_membership:Requirement {section: '31', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'A thing must be able to belong to several clusters, must never be forced under one parent, and must count for 1/N in each of its N clusters\' centroids.'}),
(parent_floor_optional:Requirement {section: '31', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Doubt must stay local by default, but a parent cluster may opt in to a floor so that its certainty is never stated above that of its least certain member.'}),
(cycles_allowed_flagged:Requirement {section: '31', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Cycles must be allowed and flagged, never refused, with no depth limit and no acyclic check at write time, and views must be able to hide or highlight the cycled regions.'}),
(derivations_need_no_upkeep:Requirement {section: '31', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Centroids, indexes, views and lens lists must be derived automatically, with no management by the user.'}),
(centroids_correct_first:Requirement {section: '31', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Centroid recomputation must favour a correct result over a fast one.'}),
(altitude_query_parameter:Requirement {section: '32', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Altitude must be a query parameter that collapses subgraphs to their centroids and the links between them, or expands them to every thing, walking the composition levels of §22 of the graph spec.'}),
(audience_profiles_named:Requirement {section: '32', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Altitude bands must be named audience profiles, each defined by vocabulary depth, prerequisite knowledge and the ratio of metaphor to mechanism, and profiles must be composable labels passed to a lens, of which a thing may carry several.'}),
(altitude_relative_to_lens:Requirement {section: '32', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'A thing\'s altitude must be stored as a mapping between it and a lens or profile, never as a property of the thing alone, so that switching lens re-projects the view without editing the thing.'}),
(sibling_drafts:Requirement {section: '32', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must capture a thing at its own band, the editor must offer drafts of it at neighbouring bands from each band\'s criteria through the AI feature, as proposals the user accepts or corrects, and accepted corrections must sharpen the criteria.'}),
(one_profile_mechanism:Requirement {section: '32', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Business audiences, such as risk, finance or a board, and teaching audiences must use the same profile mechanism with different criteria.'}),
(altitude_composition_apart:Requirement {section: '32', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Composition level and audience altitude must be stored separately, and views must be able to filter on or combine either.'}),
(browsing_leaves_no_links:Requirement {section: '32', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Moving between altitudes must not create links, plain browsing must be logged as ephemeral and fade, and only a descent followed by a consequence, such as a new link, a note, a changed query or a reference, may persist as a labelled link.'}),
(altitude_topic_index:Requirement {section: '32', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Altitude and topic tags must be indexed for exact and similarity search, ranked by the derived certainty sort key, with contested things marked, and the index must be derived and rebuildable from the text.'}),
(phase_shifts_altitude:Requirement {section: '32', slice: 7, level: 'may', rule: 'A process transition may map to a change of altitude, so that moving between phases changes how deep the view goes (provisional — built as a replaceable part).'}),
(lens_is_saved_query:Requirement {section: '33', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'A lens must be a named, saved query with an optional default altitude, stored as graph data that joins and can be contested like any claim, flagged when a schema it depends on changes, and never holding data of its own.'}),
(lens_library:Requirement {section: '33', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must ship with ready-made lenses, and the user, or an AI through a proposal, must be able to write and save a new lens during a session.'}),
(lens_freshness:Requirement {section: '33', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Lenses must be findable by how often and how recently they were run and by the topics they touch, a lens unopened for months must show as dormant through a freshness property kept apart from certainty, and a recent-lenses view must show the five most used, worked out when asked.'}),
(transitions_walkable:Requirement {section: '34', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'A query must treat process transitions as things it can walk, alongside data, so that one query can go from a concept, through a state, to a part of a diagram.'}),
(hybrid_transitions:Requirement {section: '34', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'A transition must be a link between states by default and must be promotable to a full node, joined by reserved from and to links, when it carries a decision, a review, commentary or an action with side effects, and promotion must be a graph change that joins and can be contested like any other.'}),
(guards_are_data:Requirement {section: '34', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'A guard must be a node holding a simple expression, or naming a versioned function where the logic is too complex for the expression language, and that name and version must be visible.'}),
(run_log_is_truth:Requirement {section: '34', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'A run\'s state must be a log of states entered and left, and the current-state link must be derived from that log and refreshed on each new entry.'}),
(machines_inside_states:Requirement {section: '34', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'A state must be able to contain its own machine through contains links, under the recursion rules, with no depth limit, and the inner machine\'s events must be logged under the parent state.'}),
(one_process_core:Requirement {section: '34', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'State machines, workflows, pipelines, event flows, decision tables, Petri nets, actor models and schedules must be views of one process core, never translations between models.'}),
(core_of_eight:Requirement {section: '34', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'The core must define exactly eight kinds, step, dependency, condition, decision, event, actor or role, artefact and resource, with time as properties, ownership as an owns link, roles kept apart from people, and layout and other per-view details in view settings, and data using a kind outside the eight must be flagged, never refused.'}),
(views_declare_limits:Requirement {section: '34', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'The core must accept loops, parallel steps and timing in one canonical form, and each view must declare what it cannot show and fold or mark it, so that breaking a view\'s rules makes that view incomplete while the core stays valid.'}),
(edits_from_any_view:Requirement {section: '34', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Every view must write its edits into the core, saving incomplete edits and showing the gaps, flagging what the view hides, listing the affected views before saving, catching dangling resource links rather than deleting them, and keeping view-only edits out of the core.'}),
(process_views_cached:Requirement {section: '34', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'A process view must be worked out when first asked for, kept, and rebuilt only when the core changes.'}),
(process_fold_marks:Requirement {section: '34', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'What a process view cannot draw, such as a folded loop, must be drawn as a mark of its own kind in the shared drawing marks, with an accessible name that says what is hidden (Roger, 22 Oct 2026: a hook made now, so process views need no retrofit).'}),
(running_rule_override:Requirement {section: '34', slice: 7, level: 'must', rule: 'Each process must record one default running rule, and a run may override it, with the override recorded on the run and the process default unchanged.'}),
(test_runs_flagged:Requirement {section: '35', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A test run must be its own run node, flagged as a test, with its own log, and lenses must be able to show or hide test runs.'}),
(process_debugger:Requirement {section: '35', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'The path-play scrubber must step a test run one transition at a time, breakpoints must be graph nodes on a transition, guard, step or event that can be saved, shared and queried, and each pause must show the current state, each guard\'s pass or fail with its reason, the artefacts and resources involved and any certainty changes, with the drawing highlighting the current step and failed guards.'}),
(test_run_source:Requirement {section: '35', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A test run must read from the live graph by default, and a snapshot or an editable copy must be selectable for each run when it starts.'}),
(test_runs_leave_certainty:Requirement {section: '35', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A test run must not change any claim\'s certainty by default, and a run may opt in to doing so but must then record that it did.'}),
(intent_checks:Requirement {section: '35', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Intent checks must be conditions held in the core that run automatically whenever the process changes and report each break, so the user fixes the change or updates the intent.'}),
(saved_scenarios:Requirement {section: '35', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Saved scenarios must be optional records the user manages, never rerun automatically, never faded and never enforced, and ad hoc debug runs must be thrown away.'}),
(side_effects_stubbed:Requirement {section: '35', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'The side effects of promoted transitions must be stubbed automatically in test mode.'}),
(playback_controller:Requirement {section: '35', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'The drawing must offer autoplay at the transitions\' natural pace and manual stepping forward and back with play and pause, one mode at a time, with stepping pausing autoplay, and the controller must be a graph node whose visits are ephemeral log entries, persisting as links only when consequence-bearing.'}),
(drawing_follows_engine:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Animation in the drawing must be driven by the same query engine, asking the live graph for the current transition with no canned sequences, and must scrub through history by asking for past states.'}),
(certainty_drawn:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'The drawing must show certainty visually, by strength, line weight or a shift of shade, readable without labels and never by colour alone.'}),
(drawing_holds_no_state:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'The SVG must never store data or hold authority over state, and its interaction events must be turned into graph commands by the JavaScript engine, the only part that talks to persistence.'}),
(accessibility_queryable:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'WCAG contrast ratios and focus indicators must be queryable constraints, and a lens must be able to flag drawn elements that fail them.'}),
(accessibility_lenses_run:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Every WCAG constraint the graph mesh records must be checkable by running a lens.'}),
(views_over_one_graph:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Views must be choices of how to draw the same checked graph, shown in tabs or stacked, and the renderer must know the active view and its visual grammar.'}),
(multi_member_drawn:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A thing in several clusters must be drawn once in each region it belongs to, with a shared marker, and the containment view must never assume a single parent.'}),
(view_types_offered:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'should', rule: 'The editor should offer cluster-centroid, containment, density, matrix, force-directed, treemap, timeline, radial, chord, project-plan, block-diagram and ego views over the same graph (provisional — built as a replaceable part).'}),
(views_as_scaffolds:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'should', rule: 'Each view definition should state what a complete version needs, draw the structure it infers and flag the gaps so that filling them is capture, and role-shaped lenses, such as finance, marketing, chief executive, people reviews and work-back schedules, should be supported (provisional — built as a replaceable part).'}),
(render_cascade:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'should', rule: 'Drawing should keep structural data, view metadata and central theming apart, cascading central defaults, then view metadata, then each graph\'s own overrides (provisional — built as a replaceable part).'}),
(theme_graph:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'should', rule: 'Themes should live in a graph of their own, apart from the knowledge, with several allowed, dependencies between themes as links that cascade like CSS, and each drawing combining one theme with the data (provisional — built as a replaceable part).'}),
(theme_links_meaningful:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'should', rule: 'A link from knowledge to a theme should say which part of the thing it styles, and the graph should stay free of any theme, the theme being chosen when drawn by scope, a default with organisation, project or session overrides (provisional — built as a replaceable part).'}),
(drawing_history_diffs:Requirement {section: '36', slice: 8, level: 'should', rule: 'A redrawn SVG should replace the current one while earlier versions stay reviewable, with differences described in things, links and theme rules, never pixels (provisional — built as a replaceable part).'}),
(ai_systems_query:Requirement {section: '37', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'External AI systems, including ones that form their results locally, must be able to query the knowledge through the engine and under its permissions.'}),
(ai_minimal_contract:Requirement {section: '37', slice: 8, level: 'should', rule: 'The AI-facing contract should be minimal, one query tool, one schema description and strict validation (provisional — built as a replaceable part).'}),
(ai_provenance_with_results:Requirement {section: '37', slice: 8, level: 'should', rule: 'The AI-facing query should return provenance with every result, so a model can cite rather than guess (provisional — built as a replaceable part).'}),
(ai_queries_untrusted:Requirement {section: '37', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A query written by an AI must be validated as untrusted input, never built by joining unsanitised strings, and must stay under the permissions, after which a read-only query may run freely, and every AI query must be logged with an AI label.'}),
(ai_write_scoring:Requirement {section: '37', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Each AI write must get a safety score from its size, the certainty of what it touches, whether it adds or destroys, contested areas, intent checks and its source\'s record. The score only sorts and groups the review queue: no AI write applies until a person accepts it. Permission-bearing types and historical items must always be marked for a closer look, and the scoring policy must be graph data the owner can set by hand.'}),
(external_writes_reviewed:Requirement {section: '37', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Writes from third-party and other external sources must always go to review, whatever their score.'}),
(auto_writes_roll_back:Requirement {section: '37', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Every AI write a person accepts must be a labelled bundle that rolls back in one step and shows in a review lens.'}),
(export_only_way_out:Requirement {section: '38', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph must drive real applications only through an export step in which an AI generates code, and the graph itself must never cause a real side effect, such as sending an email.'}),
(engine_never_sends:Requirement {section: '38', slice: 8, level: 'should', rule: 'The engine should have no outbound capability, and guard functions should be pure (provisional — built as a replaceable part).'}),
(export_gap_summary:Requirement {section: '38', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'An export must always be allowed once the user confirms a gap summary, the gap list must be stored with the export and its source snapshot, and each export must be an immutable snapshot, named by a version or snapshot node, that traces back into the live graph.'}),
(export_one_way:Requirement {section: '38', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Export must be one-way, results returning only as imported run logs or as proposals of low certainty reviewed like any join, generated code must be checked against the source snapshot with mismatches added to the gap list without blocking, drift must be findable, flagged and resolvable by a join in either direction, and a lens over export records must show what has been exported.'}),
(secrets_named_only:Requirement {section: '38', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph must hold secrets only as named references, their values coming from the environment or a secret store, an exception may be allowed for a single item in restricted documentation, and a password must never be written, which deterministic patterns detect and refuse, the one deliberate exception to flag, don\'t block.'}),
(boundary_rules_on_export:Requirement {section: '38', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A corporate setting\'s boundary rules, its redactions and its AI-assisted label, must apply automatically to exports, with removed content listed in the gap summary and nothing blocked.'}),
(default_no_access:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Every engine-mediated surface must deny reading and writing unless an explicit, asserted grant exists.'}),
(inferred_grants_never_open:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A grant that is inferred or not yet asserted must be visible and inspectable, but must never give access on its own.'}),
(grant_status_only_revokes:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A grant whose status becomes contested or withdrawn must stop giving access, a rise in any claim\'s certainty must never give access, and the owner\'s own access must never be contested by a join.'}),
(permissions_before_read:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Where the platform has no security of its own, a permissions layer must sit over the store, outside the notes, keyed by node id or path, and the engine must check it before it reads any note for an engine-mediated surface.'}),
(permissions_graph_native:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Permissions must be graph data, built from roles as types and people as instances with attributes, and must join, carry their status and be queryable through lenses.'}),
(role_grant_rules:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Each role must hold its own grant rules, matching by attributes such as a tag and a minimum certainty, a person must get only the grants of their roles, roles must never inherit from one another, and a role with no rules must get nothing.'}),
(permission_preview:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A preview lens must show what a rule would grant before it is confirmed, and every change to permissions must need the owner\'s confirmation.'}),
(contested_grants_paused:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'When joined graphs disagree about a grant to someone other than the owner, its status must become contested, access must stop until a join settles it, and the dispute must stay visible through a lens.'}),
(profiles_at_display:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Personal profiles must apply when the graph is shown and must compose, and what is learned over time must arrive as suggestions the owner approves.'}),
(profile_settings:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A profile must set density, chunk size, visual cues, reminders and pacing.'}),
(reminders_sent_elsewhere:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Reminders must be derived from the graph and styled by the profile but sent by an outside tool, and the engine must never send them.'}),
(pacing_automatic:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Pacing must be detected from signals in the session only, each with a visible reason, applied at once with a visible indicator and a one-tap override, overrides must feed back into detection, and a change to the profile itself must still need approval.'}),
(profiles_private:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Profiles must be private nodes in the personal layer, readable only by the owner through engine-mediated surfaces, and a corporate view must never see them, only their effect on what is shown.'}),
(adapted_for_viewer:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A profile must adapt a shared view for its viewer only, without changing the content, a label must say the view is adapted with the original one tap away, and a one-tap show-as-shared switch must last for the session, reset itself and never be saved.'}),
(learning_review_lens:Requirement {section: '39', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A private review lens must show what has been learned, with one-tap rollback and clear-all.'}),
(crdt_transport:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'must', rule: 'A CRDT layer must carry edits from desktops, phones and AI authors between devices at the level of the text and produce the canonical notes, the derived store must rebuild from the merged text, and conflicts of meaning must then be found by the join and held as contested claims.'}),
(local_encrypted_store:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'must', rule: 'Every device must hold a local encrypted copy and sync peer to peer with no server deciding what is true, using Syncthing and upstream Automerge with the encryption in a wrapper package, forking Automerge only if upstream cannot carry it.'}),
(five_layer_stack:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'should', rule: 'The system should be layered as an encrypted store, the CRDT, the engine that reads, checks and refuses invalid statements before they replicate, a read-only altitude and lens projection, and presentation, each layer talking only to the one below, with the text produced by the CRDT staying canonical (provisional — built as a replaceable part).'}),
(embedded_backends:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'should', rule: 'Derived stores should be embedded, in-process, single-file backends, SQLite for small graphs and an embedded graph database for deeper walks, or an organisation\'s existing SQL Server or Postgres through recursive queries or SQL/PGQ, never needing a server or a JVM (provisional — built as a replaceable part).'}),
(encrypted_everywhere:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'must', rule: 'Data must be encrypted at rest and in transit, and keys must never leave the owner\'s devices or cross the corporate boundary.'}),
(mesh_vpn_sync:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'must', rule: 'Peer-to-peer sync must work over a mesh VPN such as Tailscale, with Headscale, NetBird, Nebula or plain WireGuard as alternatives, private keys must stay on the owner\'s devices, and relayed traffic must stay end-to-end encrypted.'}),
(ios_app:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'must', rule: 'The app must run on iPhone, reaching the public through TestFlight now and the App Store later (Roger, 23 Oct 2026), with ad hoc builds, a wireless install from Xcode or SideStore kept to Roger\'s own devices and his testers\', with the engine inside the app and its keys generated on each device at first launch and kept in that device\'s Keychain, never in the binary, so a signing expiry means only reinstalling.'}),
(web_layer_separate:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'must', rule: 'Web presentation must be a separate, secure layer hosted somewhere the owner controls, apart from the stores on each device.'}),
(corporate_personal_split:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'must', rule: 'The system must split into a corporate layer, the sanctioned patterns and ways of working inside a corporate setting, and a personal layer of open-source engine, profiles and tooling that the owner controls and that works on its own, and the same structured content must render in either.'}),
(boundary_contract:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'must', rule: 'Anything crossing from the personal layer to a corporate one must be readable by both and must never carry private keys, or AI-generated content outside the boundary rules, into systems whose terms the owner does not control.'}),
(open_no_lock_in:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'must', rule: 'The library must be open source with no vendor lock-in, and usable on mobile, desktop and the web, at work and at home.'}),
(platform_security_first:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'must', rule: 'Where a platform has permissions of its own, such as SharePoint list items, the system must inherit them and add its own finer policy on top, on who may edit, what needs review and which joins are allowed, never widening the platform\'s access.'}),
(corporate_filtered_views:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'may', rule: 'Each corporate setting may get its own filtered view, built from the explicit base grants with that setting\'s redactions applied, redaction only ever narrowing access, and each small override for a setting needing confirmation (experimental — behind a switch, off by default).'}),
(portable_no_servers:Requirement {section: '40', slice: 9, level: 'must', rule: 'The system must work offline, on iOS, macOS and the web, and in corporate settings without installing a server.'}),
(a11y_keyboard_auto:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A scripted keyboard-only run must reach and operate every action of every view in every host in CI, and fail on any action it cannot reach or any focus it cannot leave.'}),
(a11y_semantics_auto:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every control of every view in every host must expose an accessible name, role and state, checked in CI against the view\'s accessibility tree, and the graph\'s text version must match its drawing.'}),
(a11y_targets_reflow_auto:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every pointer target must be at least 24 by 24 CSS pixels, and every view must reflow at 320 pixels wide with nothing lost, checked in CI in every host.'}),
(ux_journey_auto:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A scripted first-run journey, opening a note, reading a graph, editing it, fixing a problem and exporting it, must run in CI in every host, counting its steps and interactions, and the quality ratchet must fail when either grows without a recorded reason.'}),
(ux_copy_auto:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every message the user reads must pass an automated plain-language check of reading level, length and a list of banned jargon, and CI must fail on a worse score without a recorded reason.'}),
(visual_regression_auto:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every view\'s drawing must be compared in CI with a committed reference in the light and dark themes, and any difference must be accepted with a recorded reason.'}),
(speed_budgets:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The quality ratchet must hold each host to budgets, counted in CPU instructions, for drawing 50 and 500 things, a query over a 10 000-thing mesh and a cold start.'}),
(drawing_counts_regained:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'should', rule: 'The drawings\' instruction counts should come back to, or below, those of the 18 Oct 2026 release runner before they were made steady (draw_fifty 0.94 billion, draw_five_hundred 12.48 billion), without widening the 5% each count may grow by.'}),
(thresholds_named:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every number a rule leaves open, such as how strong instance evidence must be (type_suggestion_bundle) or the cycle guard\'s threshold and passes (centroid_cycle_guard), must be a named setting with a stated default and range, tested at its edges.'}),
(lib_github_release:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Each release must publish the JavaScript library, the reader, checker, layout and core, as a GitHub release with a semantic version tag and a changelog, and never to npm (Q-02).'}),
(lib_public_api:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The library\'s public interface must be declared and checked in CI, so that a breaking change cannot be released without a new major version.'}),
(nightly_prerelease:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Each night that main has moved, CI must run the full suite on it and, only if every test passes, publish the plugin and the libraries as a GitHub pre-release with the next patch version and a nightly suffix, keeping the last seven; a second build on the same day must take a name of its own that sorts after the first and before the next day, never replacing it; a pre-release must never count as a release in the build status, the wiki or the notes of the next stable release.'}),
(community_listing:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The plugin must pass the Obsidian community plugin review checklist in CI and be listed in the community plugin directory.'}),
(readme_status_generated:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The status line of the README must be generated from the requirements graph and checked in CI, so it never drifts from how far the slices are proved.'}),
(host_contract_published:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The host interface, covering display, links, settings, secret storage and files, must be versioned with the library, and a test host built from that interface alone must pass the shared host tests with no change to the core.'}),
(host_version_derived:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The host interface must be declared in the library\'s public interface, and its version recorded with a digest of that declaration, so a change to it without a new version fails CI and a break needs a new major version (risk API-3).'}),
(storage_contract:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Notes must be read and written through one storage interface, with backends for an Obsidian vault, a plain folder and a VS Code workspace that each pass one shared contract suite, and encryption and sync must plug in at this layer.'}),
(standalone_app:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A standalone app built with Tauri v2 on the browser host shell must open a plain folder of notes and draw and edit its graph blocks offline on macOS, Windows and Linux, and the same shell must carry the iPhone app (ios_app).'}),
(vscode_plugin:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A VS Code extension must draw and edit the graph blocks of a workspace\'s Markdown files through the shared display layer in a webview, offering the language model API of VS Code first (host_ai_first).'}),
(requirements_as_graph_note:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Once graph notes can hold it, the requirements graph must be kept as a graph note in this repository, read and checked by the same tools, and every new requirement must be captured there as it is agreed.'}),
(changes_view:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph view must offer a changes view that draws what changed between two versions of a graph, the current text and one the user picks such as the last commit or a branch, marking every thing and link added, removed or changed on the drawing, not by colour alone, and listing the same changes as text.'}),
(github_review_items:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph view must show the pull requests, merges and commits of the repository a note lives in as items, each opening in the changes view, so that the user and Claude can review a change together; a high level must summarise it by section, workstream and requirement, and each level down must show more, to every thing, link and line changed. GitHub must be read only, only when the user asks, with the host\'s own sign-in, never a stored token.'}),
(wiki_generated:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The project\'s GitHub wiki must be made from the repository on every push to main and every release: where the work stands, the roadmap clustered into phases (shipped, now, next, later and held) with each release marked on it and counted under its wave, the decisions, the open questions, the quality figures and the releases, each page saying which file and commit it came from, so it never drifts and nobody keeps it by hand.'}),
(waves_on_time:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The plan must stay true to the work: a wave still behind more than one wave length after its end must fail the build unless requirements/waves.json gives its reason, so its work is moved and later dates are shifted, with the real pace read from the Plan versus actual page.'}),
(user_guide_wiki:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The wiki must hold a guide to using ThoughtKnot in plain words for people who are not technical, with screenshots taken from the current build each time the wiki is made and a list of every setting made from the editor\'s own options, and a button of the drawing that the guide does not explain must be reported when the wiki is made.'}),
(developer_wiki:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The wiki must hold pages for developers, the architecture, writing a plug-in, adding a host and a reference, whose drawings, interfaces, extension points, contract checks, design tokens and problem codes are made from the code and requirements/architecture.gql each time the wiki is made, drawn as ThoughtKnot draws a graph, with each requirement marked as released, on main only (nightly), being built or planned, and a plug-in example on the page that the tests install.'}),
(plan_vs_actual:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The wiki must hold a Plan versus actual page, made from the repository each time the wiki is made. For each wave it shows the planned start and end, the requirements proved out of all, the days the first and last became proved on main, the first stable release after that, and the slip against the planned end in days. Below that it shows the requirements proved each day, the rate over the last seven days, and the day each remaining wave would finish at that rate. So future plans are estimated from real data.'}),
(plan_rebaselined:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A wave whose dates are planned again must keep its earlier plan in the waves file: the old start and end, the day they were replaced and why. The Plan versus actual page must show the first planned end and the slip from it beside the current plan. Each workstream must carry a size, S, M or L, shown beside each forecast. The Max plan usage of each week must be written by hand in requirements/capacity.json and shown on the page beside what that week proved (Roger, 19 Oct 2026).'}),
(wiki_reviewed:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every wiki page a person writes or edits must be checked each time the wiki is made or edited, and never changed by the automation: its suggestions (a broken link or picture, an old version, a sentence too long) must be kept in one open issue, and a person\'s edit to a page the wiki makes must be reported there before it is replaced, so it can be moved into the repository.'}),
(ethos_checked:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The project\'s ethos must be a graph in the repository, requirements/ethos.gql: each principle with its reason and its rules, and each rule naming the requirements that check it, or saying that a person checks it or that nothing does yet. The wiki must show it with each rule\'s state, and a rule with no check, a check of an unknown kind or a requirement that does not exist must fail the build.'}),
(plain_docs_auto:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every document and code comment in the project must be plain language, in the language it is written in, that suits a reader with ADHD, checked automatically: sentences of 25 words or fewer, paragraphs of 80 words or fewer, a heading at least every 400 words and no filler words, and a reading grade of 9 or lower on the pages for people using the app. Misses already there are counted a file at a time; CI must fail on a file with more, or on a new file with any (Roger, 18 Oct 2026).'}),
(app_signed:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The desktop app\'s installers must be signed where Roger has set the keys, and built unsigned, with a warning naming any missing key, where he has not: macOS with a Developer ID certificate and notarised by Apple, and the Linux .deb with a GPG signature beside it. Keys reach no pull request build. Windows stays unsigned until Roger picks a way (23 Oct 2026).'}),
(apple_terms:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The Mac and iPhone apps must keep the rules of Apple\'s developer terms that apply to them, listed in plain words in docs/apple-terms.md with what is missing and what needs a lawyer: signing keys stay Roger\'s and reach no pull request, App Store and TestFlight keys stay on his Mac, nothing people read says Apple checked, approved or endorses ThoughtKnot, Apple\'s names are named as Apple\'s, and the app id sits under thoughtknot.app (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(landing_page:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The project must have a public home page on GitHub Pages, at its own domain thoughtknot.app (Roger, 18 Oct 2026), made from the repository on every push to main and each day, that says in plain words what ThoughtKnot is, how to get it and where to read more, and that search engines and AI assistants can find and read: a title, a description and a canonical address, structured data, a sitemap, a robots file and an llms.txt. It must meet WCAG 2.2 AA in the light and the dark scheme, mirror for a right-to-left language, and take its words from one catalogue (Roger, 18 Oct 2026).'}),
(developer_site:Requirement {section: '41', slice: 5, level: 'should', rule: 'The project should have a site for developers at thoughtknot.dev (Roger, 18 Oct 2026), kept apart from the home page so everyday users see stable releases only: the JavaScript and Python library docs, the graph format spec and the nightly builds, made from the repository like the home page, meeting the same WCAG 2.2 AA, right-to-left and word-catalogue rules, with the nightly pages kept out of search engines. Until then thoughtknot.dev redirects to thoughtknot.app.'}),
(words_catalogue:Requirement {section: '42', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every message, label and accessible name the user reads must come from one catalogue of words kept by key, with UK English as the source language, and CI must fail on text the user reads that is written outside it.'}),
(language_follows_host:Requirement {section: '42', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The interface language must follow the host\'s (Obsidian\'s language, the display language of VS Code, the system\'s for the app and the browser page), changeable in the settings, and a word missing from a translation must fall back to UK English, never show its key.'}),
(problem_codes:Requirement {section: '42', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every problem must carry a stable code, as the diagnostic code of the Language Server Protocol, the same from the JavaScript and the Python libraries, so its message and fix can be translated and looked up.'}),
(locale_formats:Requirement {section: '42', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Numbers, sizes, dates, times and lists shown to the user must be written for the user\'s locale and time zone through the platform\'s own formatters, while everything written into a note stays the same in every locale: ISO dates and a full stop for decimals.'}),
(locale_never_changes_bytes:Requirement {section: '42', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'No language, locale or direction setting may change a single byte the editor writes to a note, the data of an export or the layout source.'}),
(locale_sorting:Requirement {section: '42', slice: 5, level: 'should', rule: 'Lists sorted for the reader should follow the collation of the user\'s locale, while any order written to text keeps one order in every locale.'}),
(rtl_mirrors:Requirement {section: '42', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'In a right-to-left language the interface must mirror: the bar, menus, the details panel and sheets open from the reading start, the arrow keys follow the reading direction, and the style sheets use logical properties only, checked in CI.'}),
(bidi_labels:Requirement {section: '42', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Names, labels and property values written in a right-to-left or mixed script must show in their own direction, isolated from the text around them, in the drawing, the details, the tooltip and every export, and must fit their boxes.'}),
(rtl_drawing_direction:Requirement {section: '42', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'In a right-to-left language a drawing that runs left to right must be offered running right to left, its layout source unchanged (Roger, 18 Oct 2026).'}),
(pseudo_locales_checked:Requirement {section: '42', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'CI must run every view in every host in a pseudo-locale, accented and 40 per cent longer, and in a right-to-left pseudo-locale, and fail on clipped or overlapping text, a word left untranslated or a control mirrored to the wrong side.'}),
(translations_as_files:Requirement {section: '42', slice: 5, level: 'should', rule: 'Each translation should be a plain file for its language in the repository, checked in CI to hold the same keys and placeholders as the source, so a translator needs no code.'}),
(plugin_interface:Requirement {section: '43', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Diagram types, data exports, graph steps and sources, storage backends, AI providers and the commands and panels a plug-in adds must be reached through one typed, versioned plug-in interface, published with the library, and the built-in ones must register through it too.'}),
(plugin_manifest_limits:Requirement {section: '43', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A plug-in must declare in a manifest the extension points and host abilities it uses and get nothing else, and a plug-in that throws or runs too long must be stopped and named, never leave a drawing blank.'}),
