(plugin_contract_suites:Requirement {section: '43', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Each extension point must ship a contract suite that any plug-in can run against itself, and every built-in must pass it in CI.'}),
(hosts_pass_contract:Requirement {section: '43', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every host\'s adapter, Obsidian\'s, the browser page\'s, the standalone app\'s and VS Code\'s, must pass the one shared host contract suite in CI, run against the real adapter.'}),
(layers_checked:Requirement {section: '43', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Each layer must import only the layers below it, from the graph format library through the core, the display layer and the plug-ins to the hosts, checked in CI on every change.'}),
(affected_tests_by_imports:Requirement {section: '43', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The quick test run must choose, besides the tests named after a changed module, every test that reaches it through the import graph, by importing it directly or through others or by naming it to build, and run the whole suite only for a change no test reaches or one every test shares.'}),
(ui_theme_tokens:Requirement {section: '44', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The interface must be styled through one set of named design tokens for colour, type, spacing, corners and motion, mapped from each host\'s theme in one table, so it looks native in every host and a theme changes it without code.'}),
(plugin_theme_tokens:Requirement {section: '44', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A plug-in must inherit the design tokens and may add tokens of its own for the items it adds, each with a light and a dark value, and every one must meet the contrast of the locked style, checked in CI.'}),
(plugin_rtl:Requirement {section: '43', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every plug-in must work in a right-to-left language: what it adds reads in the host\'s direction, keeps the person\'s names in their own direction and uses logical sides only, checked by its extension point\'s contract suite (Roger, 18 Oct 2026).'}),
(plugin_point_versions:Requirement {section: '43', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The plug-in interface and each extension point must carry a version, major.minor. A host must load a plug-in built for the same major and a minor no higher, and a point that moves on must refuse only the plug-ins that use it, naming that point.'}),
(forced_colours:Requirement {section: '44', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every view must work in forced colours, such as the high contrast themes of Windows, and when the system asks for more contrast, with nothing lost, checked in CI.'}),
(text_scaling:Requirement {section: '44', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Text must scale to 200 per cent through the host\'s font size or the browser\'s zoom with nothing clipped or lost, checked in CI.'}),
(colour_never_alone:Requirement {section: '44', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'No meaning anywhere in the interface or the drawing may be carried by colour alone.'}),
(progress_shown:Requirement {section: '44', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Any wait longer than one second must show its progress, and one longer than ten seconds must offer to stop it.'}),
(first_run_help:Requirement {section: '44', slice: 5, level: 'should', rule: 'A first graph should be offered from an example, with a short tour that can be skipped and opened again.'}),
(shortcuts_listed:Requirement {section: '44', slice: 5, level: 'should', rule: 'Every keyboard command should be listed in one place in the help, and be assignable through the host\'s own hotkey settings.'}),
(print_ready:Requirement {section: '44', slice: 5, level: 'should', rule: 'Printing a note should draw each graph whole, in print colours and with its legend.'}),
(board_view:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A note must open as a whiteboard: an unbounded board to pan and zoom with the same gestures as a drawing, on desktop and on phone, offline, in every host.'}),
(board_is_text:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A board must be stored as a board graph, its own block in the note written in the same notation, holding each item\'s kind, place, size, text and style, so the note\'s text stays the only store and every board edit is written to that text first.'}),
(board_items:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The board must offer sticky notes, cards, text, shapes, frames, connectors with arrows and labels, freehand ink, images and glyphs, each of which can be sized, moved, grouped, layered, locked and styled.'}),
(board_cards_rich:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A card must hold a title, a Markdown body, properties, attachments and links, shown as a thing\'s details are.'}),
(board_glyphs_offline:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Glyphs must come from a built-in, licensed set of icons and emoji, found by name, and never fetched from the network.'}),
(board_keyboard:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Every item must be reachable, made, moved, sized, connected and deleted by keyboard and announced to a screen reader with its kind, text and connections, and an outline view must list the whole board as text.'}),
(board_speed:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A board of 5,000 items must pan and zoom at 60 frames a second and open within a second on a recent phone, checked by a benchmark in CI.'}),
(board_exchange:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A board must import and export JSON Canvas, the open format of Obsidian Canvas, with nothing lost that the format can hold, and export as SVG, PNG and PDF.'}),
(board_look_and_direction:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The board must follow the design tokens, light and dark themes, forced colours and right-to-left direction, as every other view does.'}),
(board_item_plugins:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'New item kinds and templates should be added through the plug-in interface, as diagram types are.'}),
(board_item_marks:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Board items must be drawn as marks of their own kind in the shared drawing marks, so a board uses the same layered renderer as a drawing (Roger, 22 Oct 2026: a hook made now, so the whiteboard needs no retrofit).'}),
(board_to_graph:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Any item or selection on a board must decompose into graph items: an item becomes a thing of a chosen kind, a connector a link with its reading, a frame a group, and a list inside an item several things, shown first as a proposal and written to the graph block only when accepted.'}),
(board_graph_linked:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Each item that became graph items must stay linked to them both ways: the item shows a mark, the thing\'s details say it is on a board, and either opens the other.'}),
(board_link_kept:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A rename on either side must follow to the other, deleting a thing must leave its board item marked as unlinked and never delete it, deleting an item must never delete its things, and undo must restore both.'}),
(graph_on_board:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Things, links or a saved view must be placeable on a board as live cards that stay in step with the graph, and editing such a card must edit the graph.'}),
(board_relations_drawn:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Links between things that are on a board must be drawn as connectors in the drawing\'s style, and shown or hidden by one switch.'}),
(board_checked:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A board graph must be checked like any graph: an unknown item kind, or a link to a thing that is gone, must be listed as a problem with its fix, and the board must still be drawn.'}),
(board_ai_decompose:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'An AI should be able to propose how a board decomposes into things, kinds and links, under the rules for proposals and off until chosen.'}),
(board_arranged_by_graph:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'Items linked to things should be arrangeable by any diagram type the graph fits, and put back as they were by undo.'}),
(board_quick_capture:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Typing on an empty spot must make a sticky note, Enter the next one beside it and Tab a child joined to it, so ideas can be caught as fast as they are said.'}),
(board_cluster:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'Sticky notes should be groupable into frames by shared words or tags on the device, and by an AI only when chosen.'}),
(board_vote:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'Each person should be able to vote on and react to items, with the tally kept in the board block and shown per item.'}),
(board_templates:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'Templates, at least a mind map, an affinity map, a two by two matrix, a flow and a retrospective, should start a board, and each should itself be a board note.'}),
(board_facilitation:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'may', rule: 'A session may run a shared timer and keep each person\'s new notes hidden until revealed.'}),
(board_live_together:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Several people must be able to edit one board at once and see each other\'s changes within a second on a shared network, through the CRDT that carries every edit, with no server deciding what is true.'}),
(board_offline_merge:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Edits made offline must merge when people reconnect with nothing lost: where two edits clash, both are kept and the clash is flagged.'}),
(board_presence:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Each person must see the others\' pointers, selections and names, each marked by more than colour, and be able to follow one of them; presence must never be written to the note.'}),
(board_access:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Who may view, comment on or edit a board must follow the permission rules, people must join by an encrypted invitation, and keys must never leave their devices.'}),
(board_authorship:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Every board change must name its author in the provenance log, and undo must undo only the person\'s own changes.'}),
(board_comments:Requirement {section: '45', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'People should be able to comment on any item in threads, kept with the board and resolved when done.'}),
(cowork_skill:Requirement {section: '46', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A Copilot Cowork skill, a SKILL.md with companion files within Cowork\'s limits, must let Cowork read, check, draw and edit the graph blocks of Markdown notes the user keeps in OneDrive or SharePoint, giving the same readings and problems as the other hosts.'}),
(cowork_core_only:Requirement {section: '46', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The skill must take every reading, check, layout and edit from the library released on GitHub (lib_github_release), run as a companion script, and its prose must never restate the notation\'s rules as a second copy of them.'}),
(cowork_same_bytes:Requirement {section: '46', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'An edit made through the skill must write the same bytes as the same edit in Obsidian, proved by shared cases, and be shown as a proposal the user approves before any file is changed (answers_are_proposals).'}),
(cowork_drawing:Requirement {section: '46', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The skill must give the drawing as an SVG and a PNG file beside the note, and a summary card in the conversation; when it cannot draw, it must say why and give the graph as text, never a blank result.'}),
(cowork_ceiling:Requirement {section: '46', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The skill must apply the note\'s classification and the AI ceiling before a note is read into the conversation (never_above_ceiling, never_credential), alongside Microsoft 365 sensitivity labels and never in place of them.'}),
(cowork_no_server:Requirement {section: '46', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The skill must work with no server of ours: no connector, no secret and no network call beyond Microsoft 365, unless a later security review approves a connector.'}),
(cowork_released:Requirement {section: '46', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The skill must be built from the repository and published as a .skill file with each library release on GitHub, and the same SKILL.md must load as a Claude skill.'}),
(text_to_graph:Requirement {section: '47', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must be able to give an AI a piece of text, a selection, pasted text or a whole note, and get back a graph of the things, kinds and links it names, shown as a proposal over the graph (proposal_shown_not_applied) and written to a graph block only when accepted (answers_are_proposals).'}),
(text_to_graph_one_contract:Requirement {section: '47', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The instructions to the model and the shape of its answer must live once, in the library released on GitHub, and be used by both the in-editor action and the skill, so the same text gives the same kind of proposal on every surface.'}),
(text_to_graph_checked:Requirement {section: '47', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'What the model returns must be treated as untrusted input: parsed and checked by the graph format library before it is shown, and an answer that fails must be refused with the reason, changing nothing (ai_unreachable).'}),
(text_to_graph_in_editor:Requirement {section: '47', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must offer the action on selected text and on a note, sending through the rules of §19: off until chosen, the model set up for proposing changes, within its ceiling, seen and confirmed before it is sent, and recorded.'}),
(text_to_graph_skill:Requirement {section: '47', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A Claude skill, its SKILL.md also loading in Copilot Cowork (cowork_released), must take text from the conversation or a file and propose the graph block through the library, writing nothing before the user approves (cowork_same_bytes).'}),
(text_to_graph_merge:Requirement {section: '47', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'When the text goes into a note that already has a graph, the proposal should reuse the things already there by name or alias, marking each match and each new thing, rather than adding duplicates.'}),
(text_to_graph_sources:Requirement {section: '47', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'Each proposed thing and link should point to the words of the text it came from, so the user can check it against the source before accepting.'}),
(github_repo_store:Requirement {section: '48', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must be able to connect a GitHub repository as a place to keep graph notes, as one more backend of the storage interface (storage_contract), so the graphs sit as plain text files in the repository alongside its source code and other data (Roger, 18 Oct 2026).'}),
(github_repo_extract:Requirement {section: '48', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'The editor should be able to pull graph information out of what a connected repository already holds, such as graph blocks in its notes, its folders and its docs, and show it as a proposal over the graph that is written only when accepted (proposal_shown_not_applied).'}),
(github_repo_writes_reviewed:Requirement {section: '48', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Each change to a graph kept in a repository must go in as a commit on a branch the user chooses, or as a pull request, never straight onto the main branch unless the user says so, so it can be reviewed in the changes view (github_review_items).'}),
(github_repo_access:Requirement {section: '48', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Access to a repository must use the host\'s GitHub sign-in, reach only the repositories the user picks, fetch or send nothing until asked, show what will be sent before it goes, keep no token in a note or the repository, and refuse to send a note above the classification the repository is allowed.'}),
(claude_shows:Requirement {section: '49', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The Claude Code mod must give Claude a `show` tool that opens a note\'s graph in the pane, or turns an open one to the things, a path or a lens Claude names, through the same classification gate as `/graph` (cc_classified_asks), and changes nothing in the note.'}),
(claude_presents:Requirement {section: '49', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The mod must give Claude a `present` tool that takes steps in order, each naming the things and links to pick out and a short caption, which the pane walks through with next and back buttons and keys, the person setting the pace; a step that names something not in the graph must be reported, never drawn as an empty step (path_no_steps).'}),
(presentation_kept:Requirement {section: '49', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'A presentation should be able to be kept in the note as a graph block of its steps, written only when the person accepts it (answers_are_proposals), so it can be played again with no AI.'}),
(claude_asks_decision:Requirement {section: '49', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The mod must give Claude a `decide` tool that puts a question with two to six options in the pane, each option tied to things in the graph, and gives back to Claude only the option the person picked and any reason they typed, or that they chose none.'}),
(decision_person_only:Requirement {section: '49', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Only the person may answer a decision: an answer must come from their own input in the pane, never from Claude, a tool call, a hook or Remote Control, as `fromPerson` already holds for `/graph confirm`.'}),
(decision_recorded:Requirement {section: '49', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The person\'s pick must be written to the note as a decision typed by the standard schema decision@1 (standard_schemas): the question, the options, the one picked, who decided, when and the reason, reusing a judgement-graph block the note has (rejecting_recorded), and passing the change-set check (proposals_checked).'}),
(mcp_app:Requirement {section: '49', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'An MCP server, built from the library released on GitHub (lib_github_release), must offer the same show, present and decide as an MCP App, its page served as a `ui://` resource and drawn in the host\'s sandboxed frame, so graphs are shown and decisions taken in Claude\'s desktop app chat and in Claude Code alike.'}),
(mcp_app_one_core:Requirement {section: '49', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The MCP App\'s page must draw with the core and the shared display layer, with no copy of the notation\'s rules, and must say why and show the graph as text when it cannot draw, never a blank frame.'}),
(mcp_app_local:Requirement {section: '49', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The MCP server must run on the person\'s own device over standard input and output, read notes only from the folder they name, and hold every note to the classification gate and the AI ceiling before anything reaches the conversation (never_above_ceiling, never_credential), with no server of ours and no network call.'}),
(mcp_app_page_bounded:Requirement {section: '49', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Every tool call the MCP App\'s page makes must pass the server\'s gate, every change it asks for must be a proposal (answers_are_proposals), and what the page adds to the model\'s context must be only the person\'s pick and what the pane shows at or below the ceiling.'}),
(mcp_app_remote:Requirement {section: '49', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'The MCP App should reach Claude on the web, on mobile and in Cowork as a remote connector only after a security review approves one, as the Copilot Cowork skill waits for one (cowork_no_server).'}),
(risk_register:Requirement {section: '50', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every risk an expert panel raises must be recorded in quality/risks.json with the owner\'s decision: fix now, fix later in a named wave of the plan, or accept with a reason and who accepted it. Each risk names the files it lives in, its proof as file and line, and the commit it was last looked at.'}),
(risk_release_gate:Requirement {section: '50', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A release and its dry run must fail while any risk is open and marked fix now, any fix-later risk is open past the end of its wave, or any open risk\'s files changed and no one has looked at it for 30 days. One whose files changed more recently is named as a warning. The failure names each risk and the command that clears it once someone has looked again.'}),
(risk_panel_cadence:Requirement {section: '50', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The register must record when every role last looked at the whole project and at which version. A release that starts a new version line (0.7 to 0.8, a major step before 1.0) must fail until a full panel has looked since, and its findings are decided. The scoped panel runs by hand before every stable release.'}),
(risk_report:Requirement {section: '50', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'RISKS.md must be written from the register by the script alone, as one scannable page: open risks in a table with likelihood, impact, decision and effort, then each one in full, then the done and accepted ones. CI must fail when the page and the register differ.'}),
(risk_panel:Requirement {section: '50', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A workflow run by hand must have an expert panel read only what changed since the last stable release: the open risks whose files moved, and the roles whose areas moved. It must spend a Claude session only when something moved, give its findings as one issue the owner decides on, and never write to the repository or release anything.'}),
(security_release_gate:Requirement {section: '50', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'A stable release and its dry run must fail while any security alert is open: code scanning, Dependabot, secret scanning, or an advisory npm audit or OSV finds in a lock file. Only an alert the register accepts is let through. A nightly must fail on an open high or critical one. A list the token may not read is named, never taken as empty, and an unread code scanning list stops a stable release.'}),
(security_daily:Requirement {section: '50', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Each day a workflow must list every open security alert in one issue, by key, severity and link alone, as the repository is public. Only when an alert is not yet planned may it spend a Claude session, which pushes one branch for each fix and never writes to main or opens a pull request itself.'}),
(security_fix_rule:Requirement {section: '50', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every security alert must be planned as a risk in the register that names it. Each security risk fixed from 20 Oct 2026 must name, in prevented_by, the test and the rule that stop the same kind of problem coming back, and each must be a file in the repository.'}),
(spec_rules_proved:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'Every rule in the graph spec must name a test held in this repository, checked in CI as these requirements are. A rule with none is listed as unproved, and that list may only shrink.'}),
(spec_conformance_in_repo:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec\'s conformance cases must live in this repository, each a document with its expected answer, and CI must run them against both readers.'}),
(spec_no_general_proof:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'No graph-spec rule may be proved by "general": each must name a check that fails when its rule is broken.'}),
(spec_index_true:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 5, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec file\'s header counts and the paths it names must match what it holds, and a rule the code already does must not be marked pending.'}),
(spec_versioned:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec must carry a version, Graph Spec 1.0 first. A change makes a new version, with a decision, a line in its changelog and the owner\'s approval.'}),
(spec_version_in_files:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A graph must be able to name the graph spec version it is written to, and a reader must refuse a newer major version with a fix that says to upgrade.'}),
(spec_version_released:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Every release must say which graph spec version it implements and how many conformance cases pass, and ship the spec with its cases.'}),
(spec_text_restored:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Text the paste damaged must be restored from the owner\'s original and recorded as an erratum.'}),
(spec_sections_named:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Every section number must be cited with the spec it belongs to, GS for the graph spec and ES for this one, and a missing section is marked withdrawn. An editor rule cites a graph-spec rule by its id instead of rewording it.'}),
(spec_grammar:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec must hold the notation\'s grammar in a form a program can read, covering the graph line, its containers and the value types, and both readers must be tested against cases made from it.'}),
(spec_canonical_form:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec must define a graph\'s one canonical form and how it is hashed, so the same graph always gives the same hash.'}),
(spec_library_api:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec must list the library\'s operations, with what each takes and gives back and the shape of its problems. A problem code is never reused.'}),
(spec_formats_listed:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec must list every format a graph is read from or written to, with what each keeps and drops and a test that round-trips it.'}),
(spec_ai_wire:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec must give the fields of an AI change set\'s header and of a serving record, the fence marks and the unit of the budget.'}),
(spec_author_from_session:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Who wrote a change set must come from the session that sent it, never from a name written in its header.'}),
(spec_join_trust:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Every graph joined must carry its source and a trust level kept on each claim. Claims from an untrusted source are flagged until a person accepts them, and only its owner can write under an id prefix.'}),
(spec_read_limits:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Every reader, check and join must stop at stated limits on size, nesting and join size, with a clear refusal.'}),
(spec_guard_language:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A process guard must be written in a small expression language the spec defines, with no calls, no input or output and no clock, and an engine must refuse anything else.'}),
(spec_hashes_named:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Every hash must name its algorithm, and serving records, judgements and issued documents must be chained or signed so a change to them shows.'}),
(spec_secrets_and_fences:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Readers must refuse secrets in text, notes and attachments. An AI reads only graphs it is granted, inside a fence the graph text cannot fake.'}),
(spec_ai_rules_agree:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec\'s rules for AI change sets must agree with each other: one rule says what a change set may remove, and who proposed it is recorded in one place for each stage.'}),
(spec_decisions_explained:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Each decision in the graph spec must carry a short title, its date and one line on why.'}),
(spec_glossary:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec must hold a short glossary, and each term it uses that a newcomer would not know links to its entry.'}),
(spec_gentle_words:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Words shown to people for a graph\'s states, such as paradox, excuse and overdue, must have neutral names, a likelihood word always shows its range, and altitude is defined in one plain sentence.'}),
(spec_display_rules_here:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Rules on how things look must live in these requirements; the graph spec keeps only the data a view needs. A judgement is corrected by a new one that replaces it, and an AI challenges a judgement only when asked.'}),
(spec_process_versions:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A running process must keep the version of its process graph it started on, unless the new version gives a mapping from old states to new.'}),
(spec_engine_catalogue:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'An engine must be certified against a versioned catalogue published with the spec, and the reference engine\'s version must be pinned.'}),
(spec_zoned_time:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec must give a way to write a time with its time zone, and both readers must read it.'}),
(spec_conserved:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A graph must be able to say which quantities must balance, so the checker can test any domain.'}),
(spec_text_licence:Requirement {section: '51', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The graph spec\'s text must say under what licence others may reuse it to build their own reader: Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 (CC-BY-4.0), so anyone may copy, translate and build on the format as long as they credit it. The code, the libraries and the conformance suite\'s scripts stay under MPL-2.0 (Roger, 20 Oct 2026).'}),
(data_tables_design:Requirement {section: '52', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Before any of this section is built, a design note must compare ways for a graph to refer to a table or cube of data, such as a CSV, a spreadsheet, a database query or an analytics cube, and how to cache or link each when it comes from outside, and Roger must pick one (Roger, 19 Oct 2026).'}),
(data_set_reference:Requirement {section: '52', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'A graph should refer to a table or cube by a short named reference that says where it lives and which version it is, and should be able to point at a row, a column, a measure or a cell inside it, never copying the whole data set into the note (text_is_the_store).'}),
(data_set_linked_or_kept:Requirement {section: '52', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'For each outside data set, the user should choose to keep it linked, fetched again only when they ask, or to keep a snapshot, a frozen copy saved as a file beside the note, as an import does today (db_import_on_request).'}),
(data_set_cache:Requirement {section: '52', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'A fetched data set should be kept in a local cache that records where it came from and when, the drawing showing its age, so a graph still opens offline and says when its data is old or missing (gql_file_missing).'}),
(data_set_access:Requirement {section: '52', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Fetching an outside data set must happen only when the user asks, reach only the sources they pick, use the host\'s sign-in, keep no password or token in a note, and refuse to bring data above the classification the note allows.'}),
(scale_design:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Before any of this section is built, a research note must compare new ways to open, draw and edit a massive graph on the user\'s own machine, such as drawing only what the screen can show, folding groups into clusters, reading the graph in parts, a spatial index, drawing on the graphics chip and laying out only what changed, and Roger must pick one (Roger, 20 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_limits_revisited:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The research must look again at the read limits of Wave 6 (spec_read_limits) and the drawing limit that shows a huge graph as text (layout_contained, device check V-10), and any limit it raises must still refuse a hostile graph with a clear reason.'}),
(scale_benchmark:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A benchmark must open, draw, pan and edit graphs of growing size, from the V-10 huge graph up to one hundred times larger, and record the time and memory each takes, so a change that makes big graphs slower is caught.'}),
(scale_groups:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A massive graph must split into groups of at most 500 things, each named for the reader: the groups its style graph names when they hold at least half its things, else its kinds, else groups found by how its things link. The same graph gives the same groups every time, and 266,600 things are grouped in seconds (Roger, way E, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_layout_cache:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A layout must be saved as its marks, by a SHA-256 digest of what was laid out and the cache version, so a graph or group seen before opens with no layout, and panning, zooming or recolouring never lays out again. A saved layout is read back through the marks check, so a changed or broken one is laid out afresh, and a failing store costs only a layout (text_is_the_store; Roger, way E, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_overview:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A massive graph must open as an overview of its groups as marks: a box for each group sized by its things, named with its count, groups that link side by side and joined by lines. An index of where marks are must give each frame only the marks on screen, leaving out text too small to read, so a frame of 266,600 things stays under 16 ms (Roger, way E, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_painters:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Marks of a massive graph must be painted on a canvas, never as SVG, by the best painter the device can make: WebGPU, then WebGL 2, then the 2D canvas, picked by trying each, never by the platform name, a painter that fails being passed over. Text is always painted by the 2D canvas, and every colour comes from the drawing palette by style token (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_gpu_painters:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The WebGPU and the WebGL 2 painters must paint marks as the 2D canvas painter does, checked in a real browser: WebGL 2 as pictures compared part by part, and WebGPU by its shaders building and a frame of every kind of mark painting with no error. Each box is one copy of one square, so thousands of boxes take one call (Roger, targets both, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_space_view:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A drawing must offer a 3D view from its bar, never as the default. It lifts the marks on screen into blocks, with height by groups or by links, laying nothing out. The person turns, zooms and flies to a thing by pointer or keyboard, and Back to 2D or Escape returns. It never edits (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_space_controls:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The 3D view must say on screen how to move it, and every move must work by mouse, touch, keyboard and a button: turn, move across, zoom and reset. A click or tap names a thing; a second one flies to it (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_space_labels:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Names in the 3D view must face the camera at a readable size and never be dropped for being small. The picked thing\'s name shows first, then those nearest where the person looks; none covers another, and more show as they zoom in (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_space_painters:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The 3D view must be painted by the best painter the device can make, WebGPU, then WebGL 2, then the 2D canvas, each block one copy of one cube, checked in a real browser (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_progressive:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A massive graph must be drawn in steps: the overview first, then the detail most likely to interest the reader (what is on screen and near the chosen thing), more detail arriving over time, so the screen is never blank while it waits (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_layout_static:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A massive graph\'s layout must stay as it is until its structure changes: a thing or link added or removed re-lays out only the group it touched, and moving, zooming, filtering or choosing never lays it out again (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_filter_live:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Filtering a massive graph must rearrange what is kept near real time, under 100 ms for 10,000 kept things: kept groups close the gaps and glide to their new places, never laid out from scratch on screen. A filter in reading view is only tried, and in edit mode it is saved (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_massive_card:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A graph too large to draw whole must show, on its card, its overview painted by the best painter the device has, with a search box, beside its text and the banner saying why (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_search_fly:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must be able to search a massive graph by name and fly to a match: the view moves to it and opens its group, animated, or at once under reduced motion (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_expand_on_demand:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must be able to grow the picture from a thing: show its neighbours one step at a time, or open the group it sits in, never the whole graph at once (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_facet_counts:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Filters on a massive graph must show, beside each kind or property value, how many things it holds, and the counts must follow the other filters already chosen (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_explore_trail:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Exploring a massive graph must keep a trail of what the user opened, flew to and filtered, with back and forward steps, so any earlier picture can be reached again (Roger, 22 Oct 2026).'}),
(scale_level_of_detail:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'A massive graph should open at once as an overview, with groups folded into clusters, and show more detail as the user zooms in or opens a cluster, never drawing more than the screen can show.'}),
(scale_in_parts:Requirement {section: '53', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'A graph too big to hold in memory at once should be read in parts from the local store (Q-05), while the text of the note stays the only store of the graph (text_is_the_store).'}),
(trust_model_written:Requirement {section: '54', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Before the contracts and graph-format security of Wave 6 are built, a trust model must name every actor, each trust boundary, what crosses it and what each side may trust, as a wiki page (Roger, 20 Oct 2026).'}),
(trust_model_reviewed:Requirement {section: '54', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The trust model must be checked against the code, and each gap found must go into the known-risk register as fix now or fix later, for Roger to decide.'}),
(trust_model_kept_current:Requirement {section: '54', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'A change that adds a trust boundary, such as the mesh join, permissions, sync, the web layer or a new host, should update the trust model in the same pull request.'}),
(trust_plugin_own_secrets:Requirement {section: '54', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A plug-in given the secrets ability must read only the secrets named under its own id, so it never reads an AI model\'s key or another plug-in\'s (TM1, 21 Oct 2026).'}),
(trust_ai_provider_bounded:Requirement {section: '54', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'An AI provider a plug-in adds must only shape the request and read the reply. The core keeps the gate, the ceiling, the fence and the person\'s yes on the exact text shown, and the host adds the key and sends it. The provider never sees the key, the notes or the network, and the answer it reads is checked as any answer is.'}),
(trust_diagram_type_data:Requirement {section: '54', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A diagram type a plug-in adds must get the graph and the space to draw in, and no host ability. It must give back shapes as data, which the core draws and escapes, and never write markup into the page.'}),
(trust_source_joined:Requirement {section: '54', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'What a graph source or a graph step a plug-in adds gives back must be held as a joined graph: it carries its source, starts untrusted, keeps the strictest class and the lowest trust of what went in, and stops at the read limits.'}),
(trust_plugin_isolated:Requirement {section: '54', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A plug-in a host did not ship must run apart from the host, in a worker with no network, reaching only the extension points and host abilities its manifest names. A call into it that runs too long or never answers must stop it.'}),
(plugin_outside_file:Requirement {section: '54', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A host must load a plug-in it did not ship from one file that imports nothing, refusing any other before a worker starts. The worker must take away the network and every way to make code from text before the file runs.'}),
(plugin_outside_page:Requirement {section: '54', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The browser page must run only the plug-ins from outside turned on in Obsidian, each while its file is the one turned on, each in a sealed worker its server keeps off the network. A diagram type one adds must draw the note’s graph there when its style graph chooses it; a plug-in left out must be said with why.'}),
(storage_user_choice:Requirement {section: '55', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must choose in settings where their graphs are kept, from the backends of the storage interface (storage_contract), and be able to change it later; a move copies every note and its history and checks the copy before the old place is let go, and nothing is kept anywhere the user did not choose (Roger, 20 Oct 2026).'}),
(storage_defaults:Requirement {section: '55', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'With no choice made, graphs must be kept in the local store on each device (Q-05) and synced by the CRDT layer (crdt_transport), so they open and change offline with no setup.'}),
(storage_history_layer:Requirement {section: '55', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'The history of every change should be its own layer under the local store, chosen apart from where the current copy is kept, such as a Git repository (github_repo_store), a folder or a database; each passes one history contract suite and keeps who made each change (changes_logged_with_provenance).'}),
(rule_look_when:Requirement {section: '56', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A look in the style graph must be able to carry a `when`, a short condition on a thing\'s or link\'s properties, links or certainty, or name a lens, and every thing or link it matches takes its look. A `when` must run as a lens, so the JavaScript and Python engines give the same matches (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(rule_look_not_colour_alone:Requirement {section: '56', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A look set by a rule must change more than colour, adding a badge, a line or a shape, and must reach the drawing as a style token on the mark, never as a colour value (certainty_drawn) (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(rule_look_order:Requirement {section: '56', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Looks must apply in the order written, and two looks that set the same part of one thing must be reported as a clash naming both, the first one winning (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(rule_look_legend:Requirement {section: '56', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The legend must list each rule\'s meaning beside its look, made from the style graph with no upkeep by the user (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(rule_look_bands:Requirement {section: '56', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'A number should be able to be shaded in steps by a bands rule, using the palette\'s ordered colours, each step named in the legend (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(rule_look_massive:Requirement {section: '56', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'On a massive graph, each group box must show how many of its things each look holds, and the filters must offer the looks as a facet with counts (scale_facet_counts) (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(rule_look_try_save:Requirement {section: '56', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A rule added or changed in reading view must only be tried, and in edit mode it must be saved in the style graph (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(calc_in_schema:Requirement {section: '57', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A kind\'s schema must be able to declare a calculated property, written as a GQL expression over the thing\'s own properties, its links or the things it links to, with its unit, and every thing of that kind shows it (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(calc_by_tools:Requirement {section: '57', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A calculated value must be worked out by the engine each time, never typed and never by an AI (spec §24 rule 4), and never written into the note text; it is kept only as a proved cache (spec §11) (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(calc_engines_agree:Requirement {section: '57', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The JavaScript and Python engines must give the same calculated values for the same expression on the same data, proved by shared parity cases (mesh_engine_parity) (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(calc_gaps_said:Requirement {section: '57', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A total over things where some lack the value must say how many lack it, never count them as zero, and adding values of different units must be refused with a reason (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(calc_loops_flagged:Requirement {section: '57', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A calculated value that depends on itself must show as a loop, naming the things in it, never as a number (cycles_allowed_flagged) (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(calc_set_totals:Requirement {section: '57', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Filters must show, beside each value\'s count, the total of a chosen number or calculated property for that value and for all things kept, following the other filters, in under 100 ms for 10,000 kept things (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(calc_details_shown:Requirement {section: '57', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The details panel must show a calculated value marked as worked out, with its expression, and must not offer to edit it (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(table_view:Requirement {section: '57', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must offer a table view: a row for each thing, a column for each property, calculated ones included, sorted and filtered by any column, with a total under each number column, drawing only the rows on screen so 10,000 rows scroll smoothly (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(calc_measures_shared:Requirement {section: '57', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'Calculated values and the measures of data tables and cubes (§52) should share one measure model (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(intent_thing:Requirement {section: '58', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A graph must be able to hold an intent, a thing that says in the person\'s own words what they are trying to reach, with an optional test that can be worked out, typed by a standard schema intent@1 (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(intent_links:Requirement {section: '58', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A thing must be able to support or contradict an intent by a link that carries a certainty word and a reason, and two links that disagree must become contested like any claim (§28) (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(intent_standing:Requirement {section: '58', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'An intent\'s standing must be worked out by tools as counts by certainty word of what supports it, what contradicts it and what is not linked, shown in words and never as a typed score (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(intent_rule_links:Requirement {section: '58', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'A rule must be able to say which things support or contradict an intent, and the links it gives must be derived, never written into the note (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(intent_checks_folded:Requirement {section: '58', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The process intent checks of §35 must be intents with a test, attached to a process, so there is one idea of intent (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(intent_overlay:Requirement {section: '58', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Any view must be able to show a chosen intent over it, each thing marked as supporting, contradicting or not linked by a line or a badge, never by colour alone (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(intent_pole_view:Requirement {section: '58', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must offer a pole view: each intent a fixed point, things placed near the intents they support and away from those they contradict, tensions between, and things not linked in a band of their own; with two intents it can be drawn as one axis (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(intent_poles_massive:Requirement {section: '58', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'On a massive graph, poles must place groups by their balance, and choosing poles must glide groups to their places, never laying out from scratch (scale_layout_static); in reading view poles are only tried, in edit mode saved (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(ai_suggest_values:Requirement {section: '59', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'An AI must be able to suggest a property value, such as a category, or a supports or contradicts link, each with a certainty word, a reason and what it read; each suggestion waits in the review lens and never applies by itself (person_decides) (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(ai_suggest_groups:Requirement {section: '59', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'Suggestions must be grouped by value, so a person sees a sample and applies a whole group in one step, which rolls back in one step (big_proposal, auto_writes_roll_back) (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(ai_suggest_preview:Requirement {section: '59', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'In reading view, a suggested look should be previewable, drawn dashed and marked suggested, and never saved (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(ai_suggest_on_ask:Requirement {section: '59', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'An AI must suggest only when the user asks, read nothing above the note\'s classification, and treat the text it reads as data, never as instructions (spec §24) (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(ai_never_draws:Requirement {section: '59', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'No look, number or place in a drawing may be decided by an AI as it draws; an AI\'s part reaches a drawing only through values a person applied (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(discover_group_by:Requirement {section: '60', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must be able to group a massive graph\'s overview by any property, look, intent or lens bucket, not only by kind, the groups gliding to their new places (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(discover_gap_lenses:Requirement {section: '60', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The editor must ship ready-made lenses that find things with nothing linked, things missing what their schema needs, unusual numbers worked out by tools, stale things and near-duplicate names (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(discover_bulk_edit:Requirement {section: '60', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must be able to set a property on every thing a filter keeps, as one labelled change reviewed before it is written and rolled back in one step (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(discover_saved_trail:Requirement {section: '60', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must be able to save an exploring trail as a lens or a path, to open again or share (scale_explore_trail) (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(discover_working_set:Requirement {section: '60', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'The user should be able to pin things from anywhere into a working set, then view, edit or export just those (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(discover_ask_plain:Requirement {section: '60', slice: 10, level: 'must', rule: 'The user must be able to ask in plain words for what to show; an AI writes a read-only query, checked as untrusted (ai_queries_untrusted), and the query and how many things it finds are shown before it runs (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(discover_as_at:Requirement {section: '60', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'The user should be able to show a graph as it was on a chosen day, and what changed since, by a time slider (two_kinds_of_time) (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(discover_compare_sets:Requirement {section: '60', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'The user should be able to compare two filters or lenses, seeing what is in one, the other, or both (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(discover_group_names:Requirement {section: '60', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'An AI should be able to suggest a name and a one-line summary for each group found by links, waiting in the review lens for a person to apply (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(discover_watch_rule:Requirement {section: '60', slice: 10, level: 'should', rule: 'The user should be able to watch a rule and be told when it starts or stops matching, once the engine runs in the background (§40) (Roger, 23 Oct 2026).'}),
(context_named:Requirement {section: '61', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A context must be a named boundary, a note, a folder, a vault or a client, written as graph data, and every graph must belong to exactly one, its vault when none is named, so the editor always knows which context a graph is read in (Roger, 24 Oct 2026).'}),
(context_guess_default:Requirement {section: '61', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Each context must say whether same-name guesses with graphs in other contexts are shown, off unless the context turns them on, while guesses inside one context stay as §27 has them; the setting is graph data, never a hidden switch (Roger, 24 Oct 2026).'}),
(context_guess_filter:Requirement {section: '61', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A filter or lens must be able to turn guesses across contexts on or off for one view, over the context default, changing only what is shown and never the text (Roger, 24 Oct 2026).'}),
(context_names_apart:Requirement {section: '61', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'Things, types and fields with the same name in two contexts must stay apart and never be compared or joined, unless both name the same shared schema, such as decision@1, or a person writes a SAME_AS link (same_as_across_graphs) (Roger, 24 Oct 2026).'}),
(context_same_as_confirmed:Requirement {section: '61', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A SAME_AS link across two contexts must be written by a person, never by an AI or a join on its own, and must be listed in both contexts so either side can see and remove it (Roger, 24 Oct 2026).'}),
(context_no_leak:Requirement {section: '61', slice: 8, level: 'must', rule: 'A guess, a suggestion or a search result must never show names or values from a context the viewer cannot read (default_no_access), and an AI suggestion must use only the context it was asked in (Roger, 24 Oct 2026).'}),
// ── Tests ──
(test_layout_parity:Test {proves: 'the core layout source matches graph_svg.py for every vault graph', where: 'outside the repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_python_nudges:Test {proves: 'graph_svg.py honours every nudge of the style graph exactly as the core does, so a nudged graph lays out byte for byte the same in Python and in the plugin', file: 'python/graph_spec/tests/test_graph_svg_nudges.py', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_shown_text_ranked:Test {proves: 'both libraries show each thing of the shared cases by the same text and key, the schema first, then the name keys, the wording keys, the first readable text and the id, with text over 80 characters cut at a word', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/shown-text.test.mjs, python/graph_spec/tests/test_shown_text.py', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_drag_moves_box:Test {proves: 'a box dragged by mouse shows where it would land and the nudge it would write, writes that nudge on letting go, writes nothing on Escape or away from every box, and empty space still pans', file: 'packages/core/test/box-drag.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/box-drag-view.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_hold_to_drag_touch:Test {proves: 'on touch a box moves only after a press and hold, and a plain swipe scrolls or pans', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/box-drag-view.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_move_box_keyboard:Test {proves: 'M, the arrow keys, Enter and Escape move a box, and the Move choice in its details does the same, each saying the nudge first', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/box-move-keys.test.js, packages/core/test/box-drag.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_radial_spoke_order:Test {proves: 'a box dragged in a radial drawing goes just before or after a thing around the centre, written as a before nudge, and the order around the centre follows the before nudges, the same in both libraries', file: 'packages/core/test/style-source.test.js, packages/core/test/box-drag.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/box-drag-view.test.js, python/graph_spec/tests/type_cases.json, python/graph_spec/tests/test_graph_svg_types.py', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_autocomplete:Test {proves: 'completion while typing in the pane and the card: brackets and quotes closed, keywords, declared kinds and types, property names and values; offered inline, accepted by Tab or Enter, dismissed by Escape, never unasked, one undo', file: 'packages/core/test/autocomplete.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/inline-completion.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_page_still:Test {proves: 'in a real CodeMirror 6 view scrolled below a card top, typing in the card and its write leave scrollTop unchanged; the card is the plugin own widget, kept through text changes, and the write is an editor transaction', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/live-preview.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_requirements_ci:Test {proves: 'CI reads the generated requirements copy and fails when a requirement of a started slice has no existing test, a test claims no requirement, or a test status is wrong', file: 'test/requirements.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_cheap_checks:Test {proves: 'the quiet test mode prints one line on a pass and only file, line, name, a short expected and actual and the first own stack line on a failure; a change runs only its mirrored tests; the AI guide exists; generated files are kept out of searches', file: 'test/cheap-checks.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_ci_plan:Test {proves: 'a draft runs only the tests its change reaches and anything else runs everything; jobs that can skip still run when the plan fails; test file names reach the shell only as data', file: 'test/ci-plan.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_fresh_clone:Test {proves: 'a pull request runs the fresh clone only when the README steps, a package.json other than its versions and dependencies, the hook or the git settings change', file: 'test/fresh-clone.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_run_ceiling:Test {proves: 'a run that allocates without bound is killed with its processes at the memory ceiling and one line, a run past the time ceiling likewise, a run within both passes through unchanged, and npm test, test:ai and test:changed all run under the ceiling', file: 'test/run-ceiling.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_quality_metrics:Test {proves: 'the quality job measures every metric against the committed baseline and fails a release on one that got worse without a recorded reason', file: 'test/quality-metrics.test.js, test/quality-instructions.test.js, test/instruction-counts.test.js, test/timed-assertions.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_engine_watch:Test {proves: 'the build reads the Graphviz version inside the layout engine, names it in the notes and notices, and fails on a published Graphviz advisory for that version', file: 'test/engine-version.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_layout_contained:Test {proves: 'the layout runs in a worker with time and size limits; a graph over a limit is shown as text with a banner, and the interface never freezes', file: 'packages/core/test/layout-worker.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_bar_belongs:Test {proves: 'with two drawings in a note each bar and each details card sit inside their own drawing card, and the card has its own border round the bar, the drawing and the details, drawn with the bar rule in the theme focus border so it stands out from the page in light and dark', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/graph-view-bar.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_round_trip:Test {proves: 'every graphic action round-trips and undoes to the original bytes', file: 'packages/core/test/edits.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_abuse:Test {proves: 'random edits and random text never crash or blank the viewer', file: 'packages/core/test/abuse.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_view_parity:Test {proves: 'every view returns the same things in the core as in Grafeo', file: 'packages/core/test/view-parity.test.js, python/graph_spec/tests/test_view_parity.py, python/graph_spec/tests/view_cases.json', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_write_guard:Test {proves: 'a stale write is refused and the typed text kept as a draft', file: 'packages/core/test/store.test.js, packages/core/test/pane.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/block-note-moved.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/drafts.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/inline-editors.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_problems:Test {proves: 'every problem has plain words, a place and a fix where one exists', file: 'packages/core/test/help.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/graph-view-bar.test.js (help behind the badge), packages/obsidian/test/pane-help.test.js (problems, in place)', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_style_graph:Test {proves: 'the style graph is checked and every name in it exists in the graph it styles', file: 'packages/core/test/style.test.js, packages/core/test/style-words.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_zoom:Test {proves: 'fit, zoom, pan and full-screen by button, gesture and key, within the limits', file: 'packages/core/test/zoom.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/graph-view-zoom.test.js (zoom in the note, full screen)', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_estimates:Test {proves: 'estimated links are drawn as guesses, never written, and never guessed between equals', file: 'packages/core/test/estimates.test.js, packages/core/test/estimate-fix.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/graph-view-details.test.js (estimated links), packages/obsidian/test/graph-view-estimates.test.js, packages/core/test/estimate-places.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_name_suggest:Test {proves: 'names suggested from text follow the graph pattern and are unique; text suggested from names', file: 'packages/core/test/name-suggestions.test.js, packages/core/test/name-gaps.test.js, packages/core/test/add-thing.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/add-thing.test.js, packages/core/test/suggested-names.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/suggested-name-writes.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_inspector:Test {proves: 'every property of a thing or link can be seen, edited, added and removed, with types checked', file: 'packages/core/test/inspector.test.js, packages/core/test/linked-list.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_diagram_types:Test {proves: 'each diagram type is offered only when the data fits, keeps the locked style, can be looked at in reading view without being saved, and keeps the card\'s place when changed', file: 'packages/core/test/types.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/type-strip.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/table-view.test.js, python/graph_spec/tests/test_graph_svg_types.py, python/graph_spec/tests/type_cases.json', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_radial_centre_chosen:Test {proves: 'a radial drawing is laid out and drawn around the centre its style graph names, even one with a single link, says that centre when a thing is not joined to it, falls back to the thing with the most links, and a centre for another type or a missing thing is a problem; the editor writes and clears it; the same in both libraries', file: 'packages/core/test/types-radial.test.js, packages/core/test/style-words.test.js, python/graph_spec/tests/type_cases.json, python/graph_spec/tests/test_graph_svg_types.py', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_radial_centre_pick:Test {proves: 'a thing’s details and C put it in the centre of a radial drawing: saved where the note is edited, with Back to the usual centre; only looked at in reading view, with Back to saved; nothing offered for the centre itself or another type', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/radial-centre.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_timeline_order:Test {proves: 'a timeline draws every graph with things: ordered by date, by a number, as written or by links, showing every thing or only the dated or undated ones, the choice read from the style graph and drawn alike by the Python library', file: 'packages/core/test/timeline.test.js, python/graph_spec/tests/test_graph_svg_types.py, python/graph_spec/tests/type_cases.json', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_timeline_choice_card:Test {proves: 'a card drawn as a timeline offers Order and Show, saving a choice where the note is edited, and in Reading view drawing it only, said not saved, with Back to saved and Keep it', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/timeline-choice.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_radial_links_round:Test {proves: 'a link between two outer things of a radial drawing is drawn around its centre, and a routing the style graph chooses replaces the curves, in both libraries', file: 'packages/core/test/types.test.js, packages/core/test/fold-source.test.js, python/graph_spec/tests/test_graph_svg_folds.py', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),

(test_export:Test {proves: 'exports carry title, legend and source, and data exports read back identically', file: 'packages/core/test/export.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_proposals:Test {proves: 'a proposal is shown, each change accepted or rejected, nothing applied without a person', file: 'packages/core/test/proposals.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_deferred:Test {proves: 'parking writes a Deferred, drawn dashed, counted apart from problems', file: 'packages/core/test/deferred.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_completeness_view:Test {proves: 'the completeness report is shown behind the badge in three states', file: 'packages/core/test/completeness.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/graph-view-bar.test.js (help behind the badge)', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_links:Test {proves: 'every kind of link is found, shown, opened by the host and kept safe; renames rewrite links in blocks', file: 'packages/core/test/links.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_edge_cases:Test {proves: 'empty, broken and oversized inputs each give a stated result, never a blank or a crash', file: 'packages/core/test/edge-cases.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_ai:Test {proves: 'requests go only where and when the user says, within each model ceiling, and come back as proposals', file: 'packages/core/test/ai.test.js, packages/core/test/ai-request.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/ai.test.js, test/ai-disclosed.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_vendored_copy:Test {proves: 'the vault copy of each library equals its tagged release, and a hand-edited copy is refused', where: 'outside the repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_display:Test {proves: 'properties shown inline and styled paths keep the locked sizes, contrast and legend', file: 'packages/core/test/display.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_core_imports:Test {proves: 'the core imports no host', file: 'packages/core/test/imports.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_help_shapes:Test {proves: 'problems, fixes and suggestions are Language Server Protocol shapes', file: 'packages/core/test/help.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_paths:Test {proves: 'a recorded path is a Path node with ordered STEP edges, and plays in order', file: 'packages/core/test/paths.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),

(test_accessibility:Test {proves: 'contrast, keyboard reach and the screen-reader text', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/accessibility.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/graph-view.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/graph-view-bar.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/graph-view-details.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/pane.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/pane-help.test.js (problems announced)', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_install:Test {proves: 'the install leaves real plugin files and no node_modules in the vault', where: 'outside the repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_repo_policy:Test {proves: 'the repo policy check: store files, pins, no install scripts, pinned actions, least privilege, no network, no hotkeys', file: 'test/policy.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_store_lint:Test {proves: 'the Obsidian review rules pass', file: '.github/workflows/ci.yml (lint job)', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_no_vault_content:Test {proves: 'the pre-commit and CI checks refuse vault content above Public and anything that looks confidential or personal', file: 'test/classification.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_release:Test {proves: 'a tagged release is built by CI with a provenance attestation and the store assets', file: '.github/workflows/release.yml', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),

(test_release_prepare:Test {proves: 'one command bumps every version file and both lockfiles, and refuses a version not after the current one', file: 'test/release-prepare.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_release_from_main:Test {proves: 'the first job of the release checks, before any install, that the tagged commit is on main and the tag is the manifest version, and every other job waits for it', file: 'test/release-from-main.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_workflow_timeouts:Test {proves: 'every job of every workflow sets timeout-minutes', file: 'test/workflow-timeouts.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_guards_never_run:Test {proves: 'a guard that calls, imports or reads an attribute is refused by the process engine, the SCXML reader and the join check, and eval fails the Python lint', file: 'python/graph_spec/tests/test_guards.py', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_licences:Test {proves: 'every bundled library and what is compiled inside it carries an allowed licence, and the notices name them all; a GPL tool allowed in CI alone can never cover anything bundled or shipped', file: 'test/licences.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_dependency_updates:Test {proves: 'security updates are raised at once and version updates wait seven days', file: '.github/dependabot.yml, checked by test/policy.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_tokens:Test {proves: 'the pane and gql-highlight colour the same tokens', file: 'packages/core/test/tokens.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_mini_map:Test {proves: 'the mini-map appears when the drawing is bigger than its view, outlines the visible part, moves the view on tap and drag in a card and the pane, follows its setting, and shows the whole of a tall or wide drawing', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/mini-map.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_details_open:Test {proves: 'the details panel can stay open beside or below the drawing by a setting, shows the graph summary when nothing is chosen, and goes below the drawing in a narrow card', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/details-open.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_details_said:Test {proves: 'the sentence atop a thing\'s details says its kind, its links in and out, its properties and its problems, in the reader\'s language', file: 'packages/core/test/details-said.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_details_layout:Test {proves: 'a thing\'s details open with the sentence, show links in before links out in labelled, counted parts with a row for each thing, and its properties as a table of name and value', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/details-panel-layout.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_details_keep_view:Test {proves: 'details that squeeze full screen or the pane to no height leave the zoom as it was, so closing them gives back the zoom and place, and in full screen the details take at most 40% and scroll while the drawing keeps 30%', file: 'packages/core/test/viewport-size.test.js, packages/obsidian/test/graph-view-zoom.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_viewport_size:Test {proves: 'a drawing in a note is as tall as it is fitted, up to the share of the window its setting allows, and the setting offers half, three quarters and no limit', file: 'packages/core/test/viewport-size.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),

(test_stored_centroids:Test {proves: 'an unrelated edit recomputes no centroid, a member leaving recomputes every cluster it belonged to, and ids are the same after a rebuild', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/centroids.test.mjs, python/graph_spec/tests/test_centroids.py', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),

(test_ai_write_scoring:Test {proves: 'a large destructive write and a small additive one both wait in the queue, sorted by their scores, nothing applies until a person accepts it, and a permission-bearing or historical change is marked for a closer look', file: 'packages/core/test/ai-write-scoring.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),

(test_community_listing:Test {proves: 'the plugin passes the community review checklist', file: 'test/community-listing.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),

(test_storage_contract:Test {proves: 'the vault, folder and VS Code workspace backends each pass the one storage contract suite, and so does a backend with encryption or history layered on; the Obsidian plug-in and the Claude Code server read and write every note through it (packages/obsidian/test/plugin-notes.test.js, packages/claude-code/test/editor-notes.test.js), as the desktop app does', file: 'test/storage-contract.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_drawing_counts_regained:Test {proves: 'the committed baseline counts draw_fifty at or under 0.94 billion and draw_five_hundred at or under 12.48 billion, on the CI runner, with the 5% each may grow by unchanged', file: 'test/quality-instructions.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_standalone_app:Test {proves: 'the Tauri v2 app is built and its Rust tested on Linux, macOS and Windows, and on Linux opens a folder of notes, draws and edits a graph, and its window policy refuses any other address', file: 'test/standalone-app.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_vscode_plugin:Test {proves: 'the VS Code extension draws and edits the graph blocks of a Markdown file in a webview, reaching only the note\'s folder through checked calls and loading only its own files, and offers the language model API of VS Code first, within each rule the core keeps', file: 'test/vscode-plugin.test.js, test/host-adapters.test.js, packages/vscode/test/ask-ai.test.js, packages/core/test/ai-host.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
