(test_changes_view:Test {proves: 'between two versions the changes view marks each added, removed and changed thing and link on the drawing and lists them as text, in both themes', file: 'packages/core/test/changes-view.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_cc_changes_view:Test {proves: 'in the browser editor, Compare with lists the note\'s earlier versions only once pressed and draws the one picked with the note now, every change marked and listed in words', file: 'packages/claude-code/test/changes-section.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_cc_note_history:Test {proves: 'the editor server reads only the open note\'s history from its git repository, at commits it listed, through the gate, running nothing the repository names and reaching no network', file: 'packages/claude-code/test/note-history.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_github_review_items:Test {proves: 'pull requests, merges and commits are listed as items, each opens in the changes view, the levels go from a summary by section, workstream and requirement down to each line, and nothing is fetched until asked', file: 'packages/core/test/github-review-items.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_cc_review_items:Test {proves: 'in the browser editor, Review a change from GitHub lists the repository\'s changes only once pressed and shows the one picked in levels, from a summary by section, workstream and requirement through each graph drawn in the changes view down to every line', file: 'packages/claude-code/test/review-section.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_cc_github_reader:Test {proves: 'the editor server reads the note\'s repository from GitHub only when asked, by the user\'s own gh, for GET alone on github.com and handed no token, opens only changes it listed, and holds every file to the gate', file: 'packages/claude-code/test/github-reader.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_wiki_generated:Test {proves: 'every wiki page is made from its file, says where and at which commit it came from, and counts what the requirements and waves give; the roadmap groups the waves by phase and places each release in its wave', file: 'test/wiki.test.js, test/wiki-workflow.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_waves_on_time:Test {proves: 'a wave behind for more than one wave length fails unless waves.json gives its reason, a wave whose work is all proved never does, and the real plan has no such wave today', file: 'test/waves-on-time.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_user_guide_wiki:Test {proves: 'the guide shows every screenshot taken and no other, lists every setting from the editor\'s options, keeps to plain words, and names a new button it does not explain', file: 'test/wiki-guide.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_developer_wiki:Test {proves: 'the developer pages draw the architecture graph and fill every list from the code, every path and requirement the architecture names exists, each requirement is marked released, nightly only, being built or planned against the latest release, and the plug-in example installs', file: 'test/wiki-developers.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_plan_vs_actual:Test {proves: 'a requirement is proved on the first day on main its every test is written, read through the requirements file moving from .gql to .md; each wave shows its plan, its first and last proved days, the first stable release after, and its slip; the page lists the requirements proved each day, the seven-day rate and a forecast for each remaining wave', file: 'test/plan-actuals.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_plan_rebaselined:Test {proves: 'a wave planned again keeps its earlier plan and the reader refuses one missing a date or a why; the page shows the first planned end and the slip from it; each forecast shows its sizes; a size other than S, M or L is refused; the capacity file\'s weeks are shown with what each proved; every real workstream carries a size and every real wave planned again keeps its earlier plan', file: 'test/plan-actuals.test.js, test/github-project-waves.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_wiki_reviewed:Test {proves: 'a page a person wrote gets a suggestion for each broken link or picture, old version and long sentence, a person\'s edit to a made page is reported, the workflow\'s pages are never checked, and the issue is kept by a job that runs no code of the repository', file: 'test/wiki-review.test.js, test/wiki-workflow.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_community_files:Test {proves: 'the code of conduct says how to report in private, the trademark note keeps the name for the original, and the contributing guide and the pull request template ask for a signed-off commit', file: 'test/community-files.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_security_policy:Test {proves: 'SECURITY.md names the supported versions, sends reports to GitHub\'s private reporting and not to public issues, and says how soon a reply comes', file: 'test/community-files.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),

(test_version_promise_told:Test {proves: 'the changelog holds a Library changes section with the version rule, --check refuses notes without it, the release runs that check before it drafts and the release check dry-runs it, and the README and For developers state the rule', file: 'test/lib-release.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_agents_pr_rules:Test {proves: 'AGENTS.md has a Before you open a pull request list that names merge commits only, each regenerating command CI enforces, the sign-off and how Roger approves', file: 'test/community-files.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_pr_description_checked:Test {proves: 'the PR check passes an agent\'s pull request with a known Requirements line, its label, no private link and a test seen failing first, and names each one missing; other branches are left alone; a workflow runs it when the description or labels change, and the template asks for each', file: 'test/pr-checks.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),

(test_agents_read_asks:Test {proves: 'AGENTS.md, near its top, tells each session to read its To [your name] asks in discussion 825 before new work, and ROLES.md links there too', file: 'test/community-files.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_roles_from_to:Test {proves: 'ROLES.md gives the From [writer] and To [name] lines and no signature', file: 'test/community-files.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_working_group_reply:Test {proves: 'the reply workflow is started by hand only, checks the reply with the watcher\'s checker from the default branch, and posts from a job that may write only to discussions and runs no repository code', file: 'test/working-group-workflow.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_help_route_public:Test {proves: 'the home page and the wiki Home page link to GitHub Discussions for help, the settings keep Discussions on, and neither points a reader to issues', file: 'test/site.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_contribution_by_invitation:Test {proves: 'CONTRIBUTING.md opens with the invitation-only rule, the Discussions link and how to ask to join, and the README never says anyone may send a pull request while the settings keep pull requests to collaborators', file: 'test/community-files.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_ethos_checked:Test {proves: 'the ethos names a check for every rule and only requirements that exist, each fault is named, and the wiki\'s Ethos page shows each principle and each rule\'s state', file: 'test/ethos.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),

(test_app_signed:Test {proves: 'each system signs only when all its keys are set and warns naming a missing one, empty keys never reach the build, a .deb signature checks out against its key, Windows is not signed, and the workflow hands keys over only off pull requests, and the published Linux key is public only with the fingerprint the guide gives', file: 'test/desktop-build.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_apple_terms:Test {proves: 'no text people read says Apple checked, approved or endorses ThoughtKnot, the name page names Apple\'s marks as Apple\'s and says Apple does not make or endorse it, the app id sits under thoughtknot.app, and no workflow holds an App Store or TestFlight key', file: 'test/apple-terms.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),

(test_risk_register:Test {proves: 'the register refuses a risk with a field missing or a choice outside its list, a fix-later risk with no wave, an accepted one with no name or reason, a done one with no pull request, and a register with no record of a full panel; the release is stopped by an open fix-now risk, an overdue fix-later one, one whose files moved 30 days ago unlooked at, and a new version line the full panel has not seen, while a fresher stale risk only warns, naming the files and the clearing command; RISKS.md is what the script writes', file: 'test/risk-register.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),

(test_security_workflow:Test {proves: 'the release, its dry run and the nightly read the alerts in a job that installs nothing; the daily workflow runs on a timer, writes its issue with gh alone, and fixes only on security branches, opening no pull request', file: 'test/security-workflow.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),

(test_trust_ai_provider_bounded:Test {proves: 'an AI provider plug-in is given only the body to shape and the reply to read, never the key, the notes or a way to send, and the person sees the exact text it shaped', file: 'packages/core/test/plugins-ai.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_plugin_diagram_type_named:Test {proves: 'a diagram type named under a plug-in\'s id is read with no problem and drawn as a network naming its plug-in, and any other name is refused, alike in the core and in graph_svg.py', file: 'packages/core/test/types-plugin.test.js, python/graph_spec/tests/test_diagram_types_plugin.py, python/graph_spec/tests/type_cases.json', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_trust_diagram_type_data:Test {proves: 'a diagram type plug-in gets no host ability, gives back shapes as data, and markup in a label is drawn as text', file: 'packages/core/test/plugins-diagrams.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_trust_source_joined:Test {proves: 'a graph from a plug-in source or step carries its source, starts untrusted, keeps the strictest class and lowest trust of its inputs, and is refused past the read limits', file: 'packages/core/test/plugins-sources.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_trust_plugin_isolated:Test {proves: 'a plug-in run in a worker has no network, reaches only the host abilities it names, and is stopped and named when a call loops or never answers', file: 'packages/core/test/plugins-isolated.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_plugin_outside_file:Test {proves: 'a plug-in file that imports is refused before a worker starts, the sample loads from its text alone, and neither its register nor its top-level code reaches the network, eval, any function maker or a timer given text', file: 'packages/core/test/plugin-outside.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_plugin_outside_page:Test {proves: 'the server serves only the files turned on in Obsidian with their SHA-256 unchanged, sealed and under a policy with no connection, the page runs one in a worker and draws the graph by its diagram type, and a file left out is said with why', file: 'packages/claude-code/test/page-plugins.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_plugin_outside_obsidian:Test {proves: 'in Obsidian a plug-in file in the folder is offered off, turned on it runs in a worker and its command joins Obsidian’s, a changed file is turned off and said, and a link is fetched once and kept off', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/outside-plugins.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_plugin_step_named:Test {proves: 'a step named by a plug-in id holds only what it takes, its inputs are checked before the plug-in is asked and given as the person’s own graphs, its new links are proposed, and with no plug-in on Run says so', file: 'packages/core/test/steps-plugin.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_plugin_step_obsidian:Test {proves: 'in Obsidian Run on a step of the sample plug-in turned on writes its links beside the graph as a proposal, and with it off says no plug-in adds it and writes nothing', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/outside-plugins-steps.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_plugin_source_from:Test {proves: 'a FROM naming a plug-in source is read as one, never as a drive letter or a note, its graph is read as a .gql file is, and with no plug-in on the block says so', file: 'packages/core/test/sources-plugin.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_plugin_source_obsidian:Test {proves: 'in Obsidian a block drawn from a plug-in source draws the graph the plug-in turned on gives, named on its strip, and says so when the plug-in is off', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/outside-plugins-sources.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_plugin_diagram_obsidian:Test {proves: 'in Obsidian a diagram type a plug-in turned on adds draws the graph as the core’s picture of its marks, labels as text and nothing laid out, and with it off the graph is a network saying which plug-in draws it', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/outside-plugins-diagrams.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'written'}),
(test_storage_user_choice:Test {proves: 'the setting lists every backend that passes the contract suite, a move copies every note and its history and checks the copy before the old place is let go, and nothing is written to a backend not chosen', file: 'packages/core/test/storage-choice.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_storage_defaults:Test {proves: 'with no choice made, graphs are kept in the local store and synced, and open and change with no network', file: 'packages/core/test/storage-choice.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_storage_history_layer:Test {proves: 'each history backend passes one history contract suite, records who made each change, and is swapped without changing the current copy', file: 'packages/core/test/history-contract.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_rule_look_when:Test {proves: 'a look with a when and a look naming a lens each give their look to exactly the things the same lens returns, in both engines', file: 'packages/core/test/rule-looks.test.js, python/graph_spec/tests/test_rule_looks.py', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_rule_look_not_colour_alone:Test {proves: 'a rule that sets only a colour is reported with a fix, and every matched mark carries a style token and a second sign, never a colour value', file: 'packages/core/test/rule-looks.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_rule_look_order:Test {proves: 'two looks that set the outline of one thing give the first one and report a clash naming both', file: 'packages/core/test/rule-looks.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_rule_look_legend:Test {proves: 'adding, changing or removing a rule changes the legend with no other edit', file: 'packages/core/test/rule-looks.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_rule_look_bands:Test {proves: 'a bands rule gives each thing the step its number falls in, and the legend names each step', file: 'packages/core/test/rule-bands.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_rule_look_massive:Test {proves: 'a group box shows the count of each look among its things, and the By look facet counts follow the other filters', file: 'packages/core/test/rule-looks-massive.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_rule_look_try_save:Test {proves: 'a rule tried in reading view leaves the note byte for byte unchanged, and the same rule in edit mode is written to the style graph', file: 'packages/obsidian/test/rule-looks-host.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_calc_in_schema:Test {proves: 'a calculated property on one thing and a roll-up over linked things each give the right value for every thing of the kind', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/calculated.test.js, python/graph_spec/tests/test_calculated.py', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_calc_by_tools:Test {proves: 'a note with calculated properties is byte for byte unchanged after reading, and a stale cache is found and worked out again', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/calculated.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_calc_engines_agree:Test {proves: 'every shared calculated-value case gives the same value in both engines', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/calculated-parity.test.js, python/graph_spec/tests/test_calculated_parity.py', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_calc_gaps_said:Test {proves: 'a total with three missing values says three are missing, and adding money to hours is refused naming both units', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/calculated.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_calc_loops_flagged:Test {proves: 'a roll-up through a cycle shows a loop naming its things, and the rest of the graph still works out', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/calculated.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_calc_set_totals:Test {proves: 'each filter value shows its total beside its count, choosing a filter updates the others, and 10,000 kept things total within 100 ms', file: 'packages/core/test/facet-totals.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_calc_details_shown:Test {proves: 'a calculated value shows in the details with its expression and no edit field', file: 'packages/core/test/calculated-details.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_table_view:Test {proves: 'the table view lists every kept thing, sorts by a column, totals each number column, and draws only the rows on screen', file: 'packages/core/test/view-types/table-view.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_calc_measures_shared:Test {proves: 'a measure over a data set and a calculated total over things are read by the same code', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/measures.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_intent_thing:Test {proves: 'an intent with and without a test is read and checked by intent@1 with no schema in the note', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/intents.test.js, python/graph_spec/tests/test_intents.py', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_intent_links:Test {proves: 'a supports and a contradicts link are read with their certainty, and a disagreeing pair becomes contested', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/intents.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_intent_standing:Test {proves: 'the standing counts each link by its certainty word and counts unlinked things, and no number is stored', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/intents.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_intent_rule_links:Test {proves: 'a rule-made support link shows in the standing and leaves the note unchanged', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/intents.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_intent_checks_folded:Test {proves: 'a process intent check is read as an intent with a test and still reports each break', file: 'packages/core/test/intent-checks.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_intent_overlay:Test {proves: 'the overlay marks each thing by its link to the chosen intent with a second sign beside colour, and moves nothing', file: 'packages/core/test/intent-overlay.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_intent_pole_view:Test {proves: 'things that support an intent sit nearer it than things that contradict it, a thing pulled both ways sits between, and unlinked things are in their band', file: 'packages/core/test/view-types/pole-view.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_intent_poles_massive:Test {proves: 'choosing poles on the benchmark graph moves groups by their balance with no new layout, and reading view saves nothing', file: 'packages/core/test/pole-view-massive.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_ai_suggest_values:Test {proves: 'AI suggestions wait in the review lens with certainty, reason and what was read, and the graph is unchanged until a person applies one', file: 'packages/core/test/ai-suggestions.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_ai_suggest_groups:Test {proves: 'applying a group writes every suggestion in it as one labelled change, and rolling back removes them all', file: 'packages/core/test/ai-suggestions.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_ai_suggest_preview:Test {proves: 'the preview draws suggested looks dashed and marked, and the note is unchanged', file: 'packages/core/test/ai-suggestions-preview.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_ai_suggest_on_ask:Test {proves: 'no suggestion runs without an ask, a note above the ceiling is refused, and instructions inside a thing are not followed', file: 'packages/core/test/ai-suggestions.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_ai_never_draws:Test {proves: 'drawing a graph makes no AI call, and a suggestion not applied changes no mark', file: 'packages/core/test/ai-suggestions.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_discover_group_by:Test {proves: 'grouping by a property, a look and a lens bucket each gives those groups, with no layout from scratch', file: 'packages/core/test/group-by.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_discover_gap_lenses:Test {proves: 'each ready-made lens finds its planted cases in a test graph and nothing else', file: 'packages/core/test/gap-lenses.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_discover_bulk_edit:Test {proves: 'a bulk edit shows every change first, writes them as one change, and rolls back in one step', file: 'packages/core/test/bulk-edit.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_discover_saved_trail:Test {proves: 'a saved trail reopens at its last picture, and as a path it replays each step', file: 'packages/core/test/saved-trail.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_discover_working_set:Test {proves: 'pinned things from three groups show together and export alone', file: 'packages/core/test/working-set.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_discover_ask_plain:Test {proves: 'a plain question becomes a checked read-only query, shown with its count before it runs, and a write is refused', file: 'packages/core/test/ask-plain.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_discover_as_at:Test {proves: 'the slider shows the graph true at the chosen day and lists the changes since', file: 'packages/core/test/as-at.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_discover_compare_sets:Test {proves: 'two lenses compared give the three sets exactly', file: 'packages/core/test/compare-sets.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_discover_group_names:Test {proves: 'group names suggested by an AI wait in the review lens and change nothing until applied', file: 'packages/core/test/group-names.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_discover_watch_rule:Test {proves: 'a watched rule that starts matching after an edit gives one notice', file: 'packages/core/test/watch-rule.test.js', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_context_named:Test {proves: 'a graph with no context named belongs to its vault, one named by note, folder or client belongs to that one, and the context is read back from the graph data', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/contexts.test.mjs', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_context_guess_default:Test {proves: 'two contexts with a same-named field show no guess until one context turns guesses on, and guesses inside one context are unchanged', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/contexts.test.mjs', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_context_guess_filter:Test {proves: 'a view filter turns cross-context guesses on and off over the default, and the note text is byte for byte the same after', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/contexts.test.mjs', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_context_names_apart:Test {proves: 'two clients with a Rate field are never compared, unless both name decision@1 or a SAME_AS link joins them', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/contexts.test.mjs', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_context_same_as_confirmed:Test {proves: 'a SAME_AS across contexts proposed by an AI or a join waits for a person, and once written shows in both contexts', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/contexts.test.mjs', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
(test_context_no_leak:Test {proves: 'a viewer without access to client B sees no name or value from B in guesses, suggestions or search, and an AI asked in A reads nothing from B', file: 'packages/graph-spec/test/contexts.test.mjs', where: 'repo', status: 'planned'}),
// ── Open questions ──
(q_01_ai_interface:Question {num: 'Q-01', slice: 8, text: 'How does the graph mesh connect to external AI tools: through MCP, an API, or file export?', answer: 'Through MCP: a local MCP server built from the library release, with an MCP App for drawing graphs and taking decisions in Claude (mcp_app); an API or file export may follow.', answered: '2026-10-19'}),
(q_02_distribution:Question {num: 'Q-02', slice: 9, text: 'How is the open-source library distributed and versioned?', answer: 'GitHub releases only, with semantic version tags, and never npm (lib_github_release).', answered: '2026-10-16'}),
(q_03_history_store:Question {num: 'Q-03', slice: 6, text: 'Is provenance stored as graph history or as separate metadata?', answer: 'A separate append-only log beside the notes, which the engine loads as graph data, so history can be queried.', answered: '2026-10-16'}),
(q_04_live_or_snapshot:Question {num: 'Q-04', slice: 6, text: 'Do the display surfaces query the engines live, or work from exported snapshots?', answer: 'Live on the owner\'s own devices, while web and corporate views get exported snapshots filtered by the boundary rules.', answered: '2026-10-16'}),
(q_05_backend:Question {num: 'Q-05', slice: 6, text: 'Which graph backend: SQLite, an embedded graph database, a graph server, or an organisation\'s SQL database?', answer: 'SQLite, with an embedded graph database possible later as a rebuild from the text.', answered: '2026-10-16'}),
(q_06_text_store_sync:Question {num: 'Q-06', slice: 6, text: 'How do the authored notes and the graph store stay in step: export and import, or live sync?', answer: 'Live and one way: the engine watches the notes and updates the store, and every edit is written to the text first.', answered: '2026-10-16'}),
(q_07_contested_in_results:Question {num: 'Q-07', slice: 6, text: 'Should contested links change query results directly, or only guide the eye?', answer: 'Guide the eye: results stay the same, contested things are marked, and filtering them out is the user\'s choice.', answered: '2026-10-16'}),
(q_08_cycle_guard_values:Question {num: 'Q-08', slice: 7, text: 'What are the cycle guard\'s threshold and maximum pass count? Parked, not blocking.'}),
(q_09_instance_vote:Question {num: 'Q-09', slice: 6, text: 'Should instances vote on their type, weighted by certainty, instead of suggesting changes? A parked alternative.'}),
(q_10_cross_device:Question {num: 'Q-10', slice: 9, text: 'Which ready-made or custom tooling gives access across devices?'}),
(q_11_crdt_mapping:Question {num: 'Q-11', slice: 9, text: 'How is the CRDT interface designed for several editors on desktop and phone, and how does the canonical text map to the Automerge document?'}),
(q_12_stack_themes_views:Question {num: 'Q-12', slice: 8, text: 'The owner\'s open points on the five-layer stack, theming and the list of views: which parts stay?'}),
(q_13_unrecovered_decisions:Question {num: 'Q-13', slice: 7, text: 'Some original design decisions were not recovered and others survive only as paraphrase: do the requirements resting on them stand?', answer: 'Review each of the four requirements before slice 7 starts, and until then they stand as written.', answered: '2026-10-16'}),
(q_14_roles_auto_apply:Question {num: 'Q-14', slice: 6, text: 'Are permission-bearing types excluded from automatic apply? The recommended answer is yes, always reviewed.', answer: 'Yes, and more: no AI write applies by itself. A person always applies it, and the score only sorts the review queue (ai_write_scoring).', answered: '2026-10-19'}),
(q_15_stubbing_revisit:Question {num: 'Q-15', slice: 8, text: 'Should side-effect stubbing be revisited if the graph ever drives real applications directly?'}),
